# Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot

ברטנורא על משנה מקואות

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Tahorot

## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:1)


**שֵׁשׁ מַעֲלוֹת בַּמִּקְוָאוֹת.** מְקוֹמוֹת שֶׁל כִּנּוּס מַיִם יֵשׁ בָּהֶן שֵׁשׁ מַעֲלוֹת, כָּל אַחַת מֵהֶן גְּדוֹלָה מֵחֲבֶרְתָּהּ:


SIX LEVELS OF MIKVAHS: the places where water has entered have six levels, each greater than the prior


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:2)


**מֵי גֵבִים.** חֲפִירָה שֶׁבַּקַּרְקַע הַמְכֻנָּסִין בָּהּ מַיִם שֶׁאֵינָן שְׁאוּבִין, וְהֵן פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבָּעִים סְאָה שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר מִקְוֶה:


POOLED WATER: a hole in the earth into which non-flowing water enters and which contains less than forty seah, which is the requisite amount for a mikvah


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:3)


**גֵּבִים.** לְשׁוֹן מַיִם מִגֶּבֶא (ישעיה ל):


POOLED: derived from "puddled water" (Isiah 30:14)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:4)


**שָׁתָה טָמֵא וְשָׁתָה טָהוֹר טָמֵא.** אִם שָׁתָה מֵאוֹתָן הַמַּיִם טָמֵא תְחִלָּה וְאַחַר כָּךְ שָׁתָה מֵהֶן הַטָּהוֹר, נִטְמָא הַטָּהוֹר, לְפִי שֶׁנָּפְלָה טִפָּה מִפִּיו שֶׁל טָמֵא לְתוֹךְ הַגֶּבֶא, וְשֶׁמָּא שָׁתָה אוֹתָהּ הַטָּהוֹר וְנִטְמָא, דְּהַשּׁוֹתֶה מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִים פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה. וְהוּא מִשְּׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר דָּבָר שֶׁגָּזְרוּ בּוֹ בַיּוֹם כִּדְאִיתָא פֶרֶק קַמָּא דְשַׁבָּת. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵינוֹ פוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן שָׁתָה רְבִיעִית, מִכָּל מָקוֹם כְּשֶׁבָּאָה הַטִּפָּה הַטְּמֵאָה בְפִיו, טִמְּאָה כָל הַמַּשְׁקִין שֶׁבְּפִיו. וְאוֹתָהּ טִפָּה לֹא סָלְקָא לַהּ הַשָּׁקָה בַגֶּבֶא, דְּמַיְרֵי כְשֶׁאֵין בּוֹ אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה כִּדְקָתָנֵי בְסֵיפָא. וְעַל יְדֵי נְגִיעַת הַטָּמֵא אֵין מֵי הַגֶּבֶא מִטַּמְּאִים עַד שֶׁיִּתָּלְשׁוּ. וְהָכִי אִיתָא בַתּוֹסֶפְתָּא:


AN IMPURE PERSON DRANK AND A PURE PERSON DRANK, [he becomes] IMPURE: if an impure person drank from this water first, and afterward a pure person cam and drank from it, the pure person is rendered impure. Since a drop fell from the mouth of the impure person into the puddle, and lest the pure person drinks and becomes impure; for he who drinks impure water renders the Terumah invalid. [----]. And even though he doesn't invalidate the Terumah unless he drinks a revi'it, he does in any case become impure once the drop comes to his mouth and renders impure all the water in his mouth with it. And this droplet is not considered Hashokah (osculation) in that there is not forty se'ah, as taught in the end of our Mishna. But through the contact with the impure, the puddle does does not contract impurity until any of its contents are separated from it. And this is as in the Tosefta


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:5)


**מִלֵּא בִכְלִי טָהוֹר.** מִן הַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּגֶּבֶא לְאַחַר שֶׁשָּׁתָה בוֹ הַטָּמֵא, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן לְאוֹתָהּ טִפָּה כְּדִפְרִישִׁית, נִטְמָא הַכְּלִי, שֶׁהַכֵּלִים מִטַּמְּאִין בִּנְגִיעַת מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין:


...FILLED WITH A PURE VESSEL: from the water of the puddle after an impure person has drunk from it. We are concerned that the droplet which contracted has impurified the vessel, as vessels can be rendered impure via contact with impure fluid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:1:6)


**אִם הֵדִיחַ.** אֶת הַכִּכָּר, טָמֵא, וְאִם לָאו טָהוֹר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵין מֵי גְבָאִין עֲלוּלִים לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה עַד שֶׁיִּתָּלְשׁוּ, הִלְכָּךְ אִם לֹא הֵדִיחַ טָהוֹר, וַאֲפִלּוּ כְשֶׁהִגְבִּיהַּ הַכִּכָּר לֹא הֻחְשְׁבוּ מַיִם שֶׁבּוֹ לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה מִן הַטִּפָּה, שֶׁנִּתְבַּטְּלָה הַטִּפָּה בַמַּיִם הַטְּהוֹרִים. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהֵדִיחַ, הֻחְשְׁבוּ וְהֻכְשְׁרוּ מַיִם שֶׁבְּיָדָיו לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה מִן הַטִּפָּה וְלֹא נִתְבַּטְּלָה בָהֶן אֶלָּא אַדְּרַבָּה נִטְמְאוּ וְטִמְּאוּ אֶת הַכִּכָּר:


IF HE RINSES, the morsel: It is rendered impure. And if he does not [rinse] then it is pure; insofar as pooled water does not become caused to receive impurity until it has been separated, the halacha is that if he does not rinse then it is pure. And even if he elevates the morsel (of terumah) we do not consider the water than comes along with it to have received impurity from the droplet, because the droplet is nullified by the pure water. But if he does rinse it, we consider and designate the water in his hand as having received impurity from the droplet and the droplet is not nullified by that water rather, on the contrary, [that water] is considered impure and therefore renders the morsel impure



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:2:1)


**מִלֵּא בִכְלִי טָמֵא.** לְעֵיל אַשְׁמְעִינַן כְּשֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בָאָה לַמַּיִם מֵחֲמַת אָדָם טָמֵא, וְהָכָא אַשְׁמְעִינַן כְּשֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בָאָה מֵחֲמַת כְּלִי [טָמֵא]:


FILLED WITH A PURE VESSEL: We learned above that the impurity comes to the water on account of the impure person, and here we learn that the impurity comes account of an (impure) vessel



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:3:1)


**נָפְלוּ מַיִם טְמֵאִים.** הָכָא אַשְׁמְעִינַן שֶׁמֵּי גְבָאִים הַלָּלוּ מְקַבְּלִין טֻמְאָה עַל יְדֵי מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין שֶׁנָּפְלוּ בָהֶן:


IMPURE WATER FALLS: We learn here that, that of Pooled Water can receive impurity on account of impure fluid which falls into it (the Pooled Water)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:3:2)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר בֵּין שֶׁהֵדִיחַ בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא הֵדִיחַ.** אַכֻּלְּהוּ קָאֵי רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן, בֵּין אַטֻּמְאָה שֶׁבָּאָה מֵחֲמַת אָדָם, בֵּין מֵחֲמַת כְּלִי, בֵּין מֵחֲמַת מַשְׁקִין, בְּכֻלְּהוּ סְבִירָא לֵיהּ בֵּין הֵדִיחַ בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא הֵדִיחַ טָמֵא הַכִּכָּר. וְטַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן, שֶׁאֲנִי אוֹמֵר שֶׁטִּפַּת הַמַּיִם טְמֵאִים שֶׁנָּפְלוּ מִפִּי הַטָּמֵא אוֹ מִן הַכְּלִי אוֹ מִן הַמַּשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין, לְבַסּוֹף כְּשֶׁנָּפְלוּ כָל הַמַּיִם נִשְׁתַּיְּרָה הַטִּפָּה הַהִיא עַל הַכִּכָּר וְטִמְּאַתּוּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


RABBI SHIMON SAYS WHETHER HE RINSES OR WHETHER HE DOES NOT RINSE: R' Shimon holds in all cases, whether the impurity comes as a result of a person, whether by a vessel, or whether by fluid, in all cases he holds that whether (afterward) he rinsed to morsel or whether he did not rinse the morsel, the morsel is impure. The reasoning of R' Shimon is [as follows] - I say that the impure droplet of water which fell in from the mouth of the impure person or from the vessel or from the (impure) fluid, (that droplet) will remain on the morsel once all the other water has fallen of of it, and it (the morsel) will be impure. The halacha is not in accordance with R' Shimon.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:1)


**אוֹ שֶׁהָלַךְ בָּהֶן הַטָּמֵא וְשָׁתָה טָהוֹר טָהוֹר.** שֶׁאֵין מֵי גְבָאִים עֲלוּלִים לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה עַד שֶׁיִּתָּלְשׁוּ, כְּדַאֲמַרַן לְעֵיל:


OR IF AN IMPURE PERSON WALKED IN IT AND THEN A PURE PERSON DRANK, HE REMAINS PURE: Because Pooled Water does not become caused to receive impurity until it is separated, as we learned above


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:2)


**אֶחָד מֵי גְבָאִים מֵי בוֹרוֹת מֵי שִׁיחִין.** כֻּלְּהוּ שָׁוִין לְכָל הַדִּינִים דִּתְנִינַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין עַד הַמַּעֲלָה הַשֵּׁנִית. בּוֹרוֹת, הֵן עֲשׂוּיִין עֲגֻלִּין. שִׁיחִין, אֲרֻכִּין וּקְצָרִים. מְעָרוֹת, מְרֻבָּעוֹת וּמְכֻסּוֹת בְּקֵרוּי אֶלָּא שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶן פֶּה:


AS WITH POOLED WATER, PIT WATER...: They are all equal as relates to all the laws we learn in this Misha, up until the second level (of 6 ma'alot). Pits, that is those made circular. [sichin], long and short....


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:3)


**מֵי תַמְצִיּוֹת שֶׁפָּסְקוּ.** גְּשָׁמִים הַיּוֹרְדִים עַל הֶהָרִים וּמִתְמַצִּין הַמַּיִם וּמְבַעְבְּעִין מִן הֶהָרִים לְאַחַר שֶׁפָּסְקוּ הַגְּשָׁמִים וּפָסְקוּ הֶהָרִים מִזְּחִילָתָן, אוֹתָן הַמַּיִם הַנִּשְׁאָרִים הֵן כְּמֵי גְבָאִים, וְאִם שָׁתָה מֵהֶן אָדָם טָמֵא אוֹ מִלֵּא בִכְלִי טָמֵא אוֹ נָפְלוּ בָהֶן מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין, אֵין הַטִּפָּה הַטְּמֵאָה מִתְבַּטֶּלֶת בָּהֶן. אֲבָל כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא פָסְקוּ הֶהָרִים מִזְּחִילָתָן, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפָּסְקוּ הַגְּשָׁמִים, אֵין נֶחְשָׁבִין כְּמֵי גְבָאִים, וּמֵי הַגְּשָׁמִים הַנִּזְחָלִין אַחַר כָּךְ מִן הֶהָרִים מְבַטְּלִים הַטִּפָּה הַטְּמֵאָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַמַּיִם הָרִאשׁוֹנִים לֹא בִטְּלוּהָ. וְהָכִי תְנַן בְּפֶרֶק ב'‏ דְּמַכְשִׁירִין (משנה ג), מֵי שְׁפִיכוֹת שֶׁיָּרְדוּ עֲלֵיהֶם מֵי גְשָׁמִים, אִם רֹב מִן הַטָּהוֹר, טָהוֹר, אֵימָתַי, בִּזְמַן שֶׁקָּדְמוּ מֵי שְׁפִיכוֹת, אֲבָל קָדְמוּ מֵי גְשָׁמִים, אֲפִלּוּ כָל שֶׁהוּא, מֵי שְׁפִיכוֹת טָמֵא:


EXTRACTED [water]...mountaintop rainfall CEASED, and the water strains and trickles from the mountain after the rain ceased falling and ceased creeping on the mountain. The water that remains is Pooled Water, and if an impure person drinks from it, or an impure vessel is filled from it, or impure fluid falls in it, the impure droplet is not nullified in it. But throughout the period during which the rain still creeps [down the] mountain, even though the rainfall already ceased, we do not consider it as Pooled Water, and the rain water which afteward creeps down the mountain nullifies the impure droplet, even though the initial water didn't nullify it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:4)


**הַקְּרוֹבִים לָעִיר וְלַדֶּרֶךְ טְמֵאִים.** דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא שָׁתָה [מֵהֶן] טָמֵא אוֹ מִלֵּא בִכְלִי טָמֵא:


הקרובים לעיר ולדרך טמאים – for we are concerned lest he drank ritually impure [water] or filled them with a ritually impure utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:4:5)


**עַד שֶׁיְּהַלְּכוּ רֹב בְּנֵי אָדָם.** הַשַּׁיָּרוֹת הַהוֹלְכוֹת בְּדֶרֶךְ רְחוֹקָה. דְּאָז טְמֵאִים, שֶׁמָּא שָׁתָה מֵהֶן אָדָם טָמֵא אוֹ מִלֵּא בִכְלִי טָמֵא:


שיהלכו רוב בני אדם – the caravans that go a distant path. For then they are ritually impure, lest one of them drank ritually impure water or filled up [water] with a ritually impure utensil.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:1)


**מֵאֵימָתַי טָהֳרָתָן.** שֶׁל קְרוֹבִים וּרְחוֹקִין:


מאימתי טהרתן – of those who are near and those who are far away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:2)


**מִשֶּׁיִּרְבּוּ וְיִשְׁטֹפוּ.** מִשֶּׁיֵּרְדוּ גְשָׁמִים וְיִרְבּוּ מֵי הַגְּשָׁמִים עַל הַמַּיִם שֶׁהָיוּ בָהֶם כְּבָר קֹדֶם שֶׁשָּׁתָה [מֵהֶם] הַטָּמֵא אוֹ שֶׁמִּלֵּא מֵהֶם בִּכְלִי טָמֵא, וְגַם שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שׁוֹטְפִין וְיַעַבְרוּ עַל שְׂפַת הַגֶּבֶא וְיֵצְאוּ לַחוּץ. וְשִׁעוּר הַשְּׁטִיפָה, כָּל שֶׁהוּא:


משירבו וישטפו (after they have formed the greater part and overflowed) – from when the rains fell and they (i.e., the rain water) formed the greater part over the waters that were already in them before the impure person drank [from them] or that he filled from them in a ritually impure utensil and also were overflowing, passing over the bank of the stagnant waters and they went outside, and the measurement of a overflowing is a little bit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:3)


**רַבּוּ.** לֹא כְבֵית שַׁמַּאי דְּבָעוּ תַרְוַיְהוּ שֶׁיִּרְבּוּ וְיִשְׁטֹפוּ, אֶלָּא כֵּיוָן שֶׁרַבּוּ מֵי גְשָׁמִים שֶׁבָּאוּ אַחַר כָּךְ עַל הַמַּיִם שֶׁהָיוּ בָהֶן בִּשְׁעַת טֻמְאָתָן, נִטְהֲרוּ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא שָׁטְפוּ וְיָצְאוּ לַחוּץ:


רבו – not according to the School of Shammai that required both of them, that they formed the greater part and overflowed, but since the rain waters formed the greater part that they came afterwards on the water that were there in them at the time of their ritual defilement, they were purified, een though they didn’t overflow and go outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר שָׁטְפוּ.** מֵי גְשָׁמִים עַל גְּדוֹתָיו שֶׁל גֶּבֶא וְיָצְאוּ לַחוּץ, נִטְהַר הַגֶּבֶא:


ר' שמעון אומר מששטפו – [from when] the rain waters [overflow] the banks/shore of the stagnant waters/puddle, and went outside, the stagnant waters/puddle is purified.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:5)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא רַבּוּ.** מֵי הַגְּשָׁמִים שֶׁבָּאוּ אַחֲרֵי כֵן עַל הַמַּיִם שֶׁהָיוּ בַגֶּבֶא בִשְׁעַת טֻמְאָתָן:


אע"פ שלא רבו – the rain waters that came afterwards on the waters that were I the stagnant waters/puddle at the time of their ritual defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:6)


**כְּשֵׁרִים לַחַלָּה.** מֵי גְבָאִים הַלָּלוּ דִתְנַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין, כְּשֵׁרִים לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהֶן לַחַלָּה, לָלוּשׁ וּלְבַשֵּׁל בָּהֶן חַלָּה:


כשרים לחלה – these stagnant waters/puddles as it is taught in our Mishnah, are kosher/fit to use them for Hallah, to knead and to cook in them Hallah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:5:7)


**וְלִטֹּל מֵהֶן לַיָּדָיִם.** לְכָל דָּבָר שֶׁצָּרִיךְ נְטִילַת יָדַיִם:


וליטול מהן ידים – for everything that requires the ritual washing of the hands.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:6:1)


**מֵי תַמְצִיּוֹת שֶׁלֹּא פָסְקוּ.** הֶהָרִים מִזְּחִילָתָן וַעֲדַיִן הֵן מְבַצְבְּצִין מֵימֵי הַגְּשָׁמִים שֶׁיָּרְדוּ עֲלֵיהֶן, אֵלּוּ מְבַטְּלִים הַטִּפָּה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה הַטְּמֵאָה שֶׁהָיְתָה בַגֶּבֶא. לְפִיכָךְ שָׁתָה טָמֵא וְשָׁתָה טָהוֹר כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא פָסְקוּ, טָהוֹר, דְּמַיִם טְמֵאִים שֶׁנָּפְלוּ שָׁם, נִטְהֲרוּ. וְזוֹ הִיא הַמַּעֲלָה, דְּהָנֵי דִלְעֵיל לֹא עָלְתָה טְבִילָה לַמַּיִם הַטְּמֵאִים, וְאִלּוּ הָכָא עָלְתָה. אֲבָל אֵין כְּשֵׁרִים לִטְבִילַת אָדָם וְכֵלִים וְיָדַיִם:


מי תמצית שלא פסקו – the mountains [did not cease] from their flowing, and still they burst forth from the rain water that fell upon them, these nullify the first ritually impure drop that was in the stagnant waters/puddle. Therefore, if a ritually impure person drank and ritually pure person drank, all the while that they (i.e., the flowing) did not cease, [the individual] is ritually pure, for ritually impure water that fell there, was purified, and this is the superiority, for those [mentioned] above, their ritual immersion was not effective for ritually impure waters, whereas here, it was successful. But they are not kosher/fit for the immersion of humans and utensils and hands.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:1)


**טוֹבְלִין.** בְּנֵי אָדָם כָּל גּוּפוֹ:


טובלים – the entire body of a person.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:2)


**וּמַטְבִּילִין.** הַכֵּלִים, וְהַיָּדַיִם לְמִידִי דְבָעֵי טְבִילַת יָדַיִם וְלֹא סַגִּי בִנְטִילָה, כְּגוֹן לַקֹּדֶשׁ:


ומטבילין – the utensils, but the hands at all that require ritual immersion of the hands, and washing them are not enough, as for example for something Holy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:3)


**מַעְיָן.** שֶׁמֵּימָיו נוֹבְעִים, וְהַמַּיִם הַנּוֹבְעִים מְעַטִּים מִן הַמַּיִם שְׁאוּבִין שֶׁשָּׁפְכוּ בְתוֹכוֹ:


מעין – that its waters bubble/flow a little bit from the drawn water that they pour into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:4)


**שָׁוֶה לַמִּקְוֶה לְטַהֵר בְּאַשְׁבֹּרֶן.** וְלֹא בְזוֹחֲלִין. דְּקַיְמָא לָן מַעְיָן מְטַהֵר בְּזוֹחֲלִין וּמִקְוֶה מְטַהֵר בְּאַשְׁבֹּרֶן, וְהַאי מִשּׁוּם מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים מְרֻבִּים שֶׁבּוֹ שָׁוֶה לַמִּקְוֶה לְטַהֵר בְּאַשְׁבֹּרֶן וְלֹא בְזוֹחֲלִין:


שוה למקוה לטהר באשבורן – but not in moving waters (i.e., rivers and springs) but a Mikveh/ritual bath purifies in an a collection of water in one place that does not flow/אשבורן And this is because of the drawn waters that are greater that are in it are equivalent to a Mikveh/ritual bath to purify in a collection of waters in one place but not in moving waters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:7:5)


**וְלַמַּעְיָן לְהַטְבִּיל בּוֹ בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא.** וְלֹא בָעֵי אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה כְּמִקְוֶה, דְּאֵין מִקְוֶה מְטַהֵר בְּפָחוֹת מֵאַרְבָּעִים סְאָה, וְהַאי מְטַהֵר בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא כְמַעְיָן:


ולמעין להטיל בו בכל שהוא – and it does not require forty Seah like a ritual bath/Mikveh, for the Mikveh does not purify with any less than forty Seah and this purifies with a little bit like a spring.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:1)


**מַיִם מֻכִּין.** מַיִם נוֹבְעִים שֶׁהֵן מְלוּחִים אוֹ חַמִּים:


מים מוכים – waters that flow that are salty or warm.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:2)


**שֶׁהֵן מְטַהֲרִים בְּזוֹחֲלִין.** וְאִלּוּ מַעְיָן שֶׁמֵּימָיו מֻעָטִים דִּלְעֵיל אֵינוֹ מְטַהֵר בְּזוֹחֲלִין אֶלָּא בְאַשְׁבֹּרֶן:


שהן מטהרים בזוחלין – for whereas a spring, whose waters are few as above, it doesn’t purify in moving waters but rather in a collection of water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:3)


**מַיִם חַיִּים.** נוֹבְעִים שֶׁאֵינָן מֻכִּין:


מים חיים – that flow but are not salty/afflicted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:4)


**שֶׁהֵן טְבִילָה לַזָּבִים.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) וְרָחַץ אֶת בְּשָׂרוֹ בְּמַיִם חַיִּים:


שהן טבילה לזבים – as it is written concerning it (Leviticus 15:13): “[When one with a discharge becomes purified of his discharge, he shall count off seven days for his purification, wash his clothes,] and bathe his body In fresh water.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:5)


**וְהַזָּיָה לַמְצֹרָעִים.** דִּכְתִיב בְּהוּ (שם יד) אֶל כְּלִי חֶרֶשׂ עַל מַיִם חַיִּים:


והזייה למצורעים – as it is written regarding them (Leviticus 14:5): “The priest shall order one of the birds slaughtered] over fresh water in an earthen vessel.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 1:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/1:8:6)


**וּכְשֵׁרִים לְקַדֵּשׁ בָּהֶן מֵי חַטָּאת.** דִּכְתִיב בְּהוּ (במדבר יט) מַיִם חַיִּים אֶל כֶּלִי:


בשרם לקדש בהן מי חטאת – as it is written regarding them (Numbers 19:17): “[Some of the ashes of the fire of purification shall be taken for the impure portion,] and fresh water shall be added to them in a vessel.”



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:1:1)


**הַטָּמֵא שֶׁיָּרַד לִטְבֹּל.** סְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא. דְּאוֹקֵי גַבְרָא אַחֶזְקָתֵיהּ וּבְחֶזְקַת טֻמְאָה הוּא:


הטמא שירד לטבול. ספיקו טמא – that a person establishes his status but he has the presumption of [being in] ritual impurity.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:1)


**בֵּין בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד בֵּין בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים טְמֵאוֹת.** אַף עַל גַּב דִּסְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר, הֲרֵי סְפֵק טָהֳרָה הוּא וְלֹא סְפֵק טֻמְאָה:


בין ברשות היחיד בין ברה"ר טמארות – even though that doubtful ritual impurity in the public domain, its doubt is presumed to be [a status] of ritual purity, this is doubtful ritual purity and not doubtful ritual impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:2)


**בְּטֻמְאָה חֲמוּרָה.** בְּאַב הַטֻּמְאָה שֶׁמְּטַמֵּאתוֹ מִן הַתּוֹרָה:


בטומאה חמורה – it is a primary source of ritual impurity that defiles him from the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:3)


**טֻמְאָה קַלָּה.** דְּרַבָּנָן:


טומאה קלה – of the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:4)


**אָכַל אֳכָלִים טְמֵאִים.** אָכַל חֲצִי פְרָס מֵאֳכָלִים טְמֵאִים, אוֹ שָׁתָה רְבִיעִית מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין, נִפְסַל גּוּפוֹ מִלֶּאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה, וּפוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה מִדְּרַבָּנָן. וְכֻלָּן מִשְּׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר דָּבָר שֶׁגָּזְרוּ בוֹ חֲכָמִים, וְכֻלָּן מְפֹרָשִׁים פֶּרֶק קַמָּא דְשַׁבָּת:


אכל אוכלים טמאים – he ate one-half of a piece from ritually impure food or drank a quarter [of a LOG] (i.e., the liquid measurement of the displacement of one-and-one-half eggs) of ritually impure liquid, his body is invalidated from consuming Terumah/heave-offering [if he is a Kohen], and all of this is explained in the first chapter of Tractate Shabbat (see Mishnah 4 in the Bartenura commentary in the first of the eighteen decrees).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:5)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ טָבַל.** דְּהַשְׁתָּא אִיכָּא תַרְתֵּי לְרִיעוּתָא, שֶׁמָּא לֹא טָבַל כְּלָל, וַאֲפִלּוּ טָבַל, שֶׁמָּא קֹדֶם טְבִילָה נִתְחַסֵּר, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר:


ואפילו טבל – and now there are two causes for suspicion or doubt; perhaps he did not immerse [in a Mikveh] at all and even if he had immersed [in a Mikveh] perhaps prior to immersion it was lacking [enough water]. Nevertheless, his doubt [regarding his actions leaves him] in a status of being ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:6)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי מְטַמֵּא.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בְטֻמְאָה דְרַבָּנָן. מִשּׁוּם דְּגַבְרָא בְחֶזְקַת טֻמְאָה:


רבי יוסי מטמא – and even with ritual impurity according to the Rabbis. Because a person is in the presumptive status of being ritually impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:2:7)


**אֲבָל סְפֵקוֹ לִטָּמֵא וּלְטַמֵּא.** כְּגוֹן סָפֵק אָכַל אֳכָלִים טְמֵאִים אוֹ לֹא אָכַל, הֲרֵי סְפֵקוֹ לִטָּמֵא, אוֹ וַדַּאי אָכַל אֳכָלִים טְמֵאִים, סָפֵק נָגַע בְּכִכָּר שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע, הֲרֵי סְפֵקוֹ לְטַמֵּא, בְּהָנֵי מְטַהֵר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי, דְּאֵין כָּאן חֶזְקַת טֻמְאָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אבל ספיקו ליטמא ולטמא – as for example, if he at ritually impure [foods] or he didn’t eat, behold his doubt [of status] is to be considered ritually defiled. Or, he certainly ate ritually impure foods, but there is doubt if he made contact with a loaf of heave-offering or didn’t make contact with it, his doubtful status is to cause ritual impurity, that in these, Rabbi Yossi makes him ritually pure, for there is no presumption here of ritual impurity. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:1)


**סְפֵק מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים שֶׁטִּהֲרוּ חֲכָמִים.** בְּמַסֶּכֶת טָהֳרוֹת פֶּרֶק הַזּוֹרֵק טֻמְאָה, תָּמָן תְּנִינַן, אֵלּוּ סְפֵקוֹת שֶׁטִּהֲרוּ חֲכָמִים סָפֵק מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין לַמִּקְוֶה:


ספק מים שאובים שטיהרו חכמים – In Tractate Tohorot, Chapter “He Who Threw Something Unclean [from Place to Place – chapter 4, Mishnah 7] there it teaches: “These are the doubtful conditions which the Sages declared clean: A doubt concerning drawn water [that falls into] the ritual bath.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:2)


**סָפֵק נָפְלוּ.** שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים בְּמִקְוֶה חָסֵר:


ספק נפלו – three LOG (i.e., a LOG equals the volume of six eggs) of drawn water in a Mikveh missing [its full complement of forty Seah]/


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:3)


**סָפֵק יֶשׁ בָּהֶן אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה.** כְּלוֹמַר סָפֵק הָיָה בַמִּקְוֶה אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה כְשֵׁרִים קֹדֶם שֶׁנָּפְלוּ הַשְּׁאוּבִין, דְּשׁוּב אֵין הַשְּׁאוּבִין פּוֹסְלִין אוֹתוֹ:


ספק יש בהן ארבעים סאה – that is to say, there is doubt that it were forty Kosher Seah [of water] in the Mikveh/ritual bath prior to the drawn [waters] that fell in, for furthermore, the drawn [waters] do not invalidate it (i.e., since it is lacking the required forty Seah to begin with).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:4)


**שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ בַמֶּה יִתְלֶה.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ מָקוֹם לִתְלוֹת נְפִילָתָן שֶׁלֹּא גָרְמוּ לוֹ פְסוּל:


שיש לו במה יתלה – that it has a place to depend upon their falling that they did not cause it to become invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:3:5)


**שֶׁאֵין לוֹ בַמֶּה יִתְלֶה.** דְּמַה נַּפְשָׁךְ בְּהֵי מִנַּיְהוּ דְנָפַל פְּסָלוּהוּ, הִלְכָּךְ שְׁנֵיהֶם פְּסוּלִין. וְהַאי דִמְטַהֲרִינַן הָכָא סְפֵק מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ בַמֶּה לִתְלוֹת, הַיְנוּ מִשּׁוּם דִּשְׁאוּבִין דְּרַבָּנָן. וְהָא דְתַנְיָא בְתוֹרַת כֹּהֲנִים אַךְ מַעְיָן וּבוֹר מִקְוֵה מַיִם, מַה מַּעְיָן בִּידֵי שָׁמַיִם אַף מִקְוֶה בִידֵי שָׁמַיִם, אַסְמַכְתָּא בְעָלְמָא הִיא:


שאין לו במה יתלה – that whichever way you turn, in which of them that it fell, it invalidates it, therefore, both of them are invalid. But that which we purify here – it is doubtful if is drawn water in the place where he has upon which to depend, that is because the [notion of] drawn [water] is from the Rabbbis. And it is taught in a Baraita in Torat Kohanim (i.e., the Halakhic Midrash of SIfra on the Book of Leviticus: - see Leviticus 11:36): “However, a spring or cistern [in which water is collected shall be pure, but whoever touches a such a carcass in it shall be impure],” that just as a spring is in the hands of Heaven, so also is a ritual bath/Mikveh in the hands of heaven, it is a mere support but not an absolute proof.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:4:1)


**בַּתְּחִלָּה.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בַמִּקְוֶה מַיִם כְּלָל כְּשֶׁנָּפַל בּוֹ רְבִיעִית מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין, וְנִשְׁלַם אַחַר כָּךְ שִׁעוּרוֹ בְמֵי גְשָׁמִים:


בתחילה – [at first], there wasn’t any water in the Mikveh/ritual bath at all when a quarter [of a LOG] of drawn water fell into it, and afterwards, its measure was completed with rain water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:4:2)


**עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם.** לְאַחַר שֶׁהָיוּ בוֹ הַרְבֵּה מַיִם כְּשֵׁרִים:


על פני המים – after that there was a great deal of kosher water [in the Mikveh, drawn water fell into it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:4:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים – and the Halakha is according to the Sages (and see Tractate Eduyot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 3).



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:5:1)


**שֶׁל לֹג לֹג.** לֹג אֶחָד בְּכָל גֻּמָּא:


של לוג לוג – one LOG in each hole/indentation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:5:2)


**עַד שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעוּ לְגֻמָּא שְׁלִישִׁית כָּשֵׁר.** דַּהֲוֵי לֵיהּ כְּמִקְוֶה שָׁלֵם שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לְתוֹכוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין דְּלֹא פְסָלוּהוּ, וְהָכָא נַמִּי הוֹאִיל וְהָיוּ בַמִּקְוֶה אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה קֹדֶם שֶׁהִגִּיעוּ הַמַּיִם לְגֻמָּא שְׁלִישִׁית, לֹא נִפְסַל הַמִּקְוֶה:


שלא הגיעו לגומא שלישית כשר – for it is like for him a complete Mikveh in which three LOG of drawn water fell into it that did not invalidate it, but here also, in a Mikveh/ritual bath of forty Seah prior to the [drawn] water arriving into the third hole/indentation, the Mikveh was not invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:5:3)


**כְּמִקְוֶה סָמוּךְ לְמִקְוֶה.** כְּמִקְוֶה כָשֵׁר הַסָּמוּךְ לְשָׁאוּב, שֶׁזֶּה עוֹמֵד בְּכַשְׁרוּתוֹ וְזֶה עוֹמֵד בְּפַסְלוּתוֹ, וְהָכָא נַמִּי הַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּגֻּמּוֹת כְּעוֹמְדִים בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָם חֲשִׁיבֵי וְלֹא כִמְעֹרָבִין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


סמוך למקוה – is like a kosher ritual bath/Mikveh that is adjacent to [water] that is drawn, that this stands/remains in its kosher/fit status and this [other body of water] remains in its invalid status, but here also, the water that is in the holes is like they are considered as standing on their own and not as combined/mixed. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:6:1)


**הַמְסַנֵּק.** כְּמוֹ הַמְסַלֵּק. שֶׁמְּסַלֵּק אֶת הַטִּיט לְצַד אֶחָד. וְלֹא שֶׁתָּלַשׁ הַטִּיט מִן הַמַּיִם, אֶלָּא בְתוֹךְ הַמַּיִם סִלְּקוֹ לְצַד אֶחָד. שֶׁאִם תְּלָשׁוֹ מִן הַמַּיִם נֶחֱשָׁב כְּשָׁאוּב, וְאִם מָשְׁכוּ מִמֶּנּוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה. וְדַוְקָא כְשֶׁאֵין בַּמִּקְוֶה אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה, הוּא דִשְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין פּוֹסְלִין אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה. אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ בוֹ אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה, אֵין כָּל מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין שֶׁבָּעוֹלָם פּוֹסְלִין אוֹתוֹ:


המסנק (if one presses/scrapes up [the mud] against the sides of the pool) – like removing [to the corners] (see Tractate Shabbat, Chapter 20, Mishnah 4) – that he removes the mud to one side. But not that he detached the mud from the water, but rather within the water, he removed it to one side. For if he had removed it (i.e., the mud) from the water, it would be considered like it is drawn [water], and if they pulled from it three LOG, it invalidates the Mikveh. But this is specifically when there aren’t forty Seah in the Mikveh, this is when the three LOG of drawn water invalidates the Mikveh, but if there were forty Seah in it (i.e., the Mikveh), even all the drawn water in the world would not invalidate it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:6:2)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מַכְשִׁיר.** מִשּׁוּם דְּאֵין מִתְכַּוֵּן לַשְּׁאִיבָה אֶלָּא לְסַלֵּק אֶת הַטִּיט לְמָקוֹם אֶחָד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ור' שמעון מכשיר – because he does not intend for drawing [water] but rather to remove the mud to another place. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:1)


**לְנַגְּבָן.** תַּרְגּוּם וְהִנֵּה חָרְבוּ, וְהָא נְגוּבוּ. וְדַוְקָא לְנַגְּבָן, שֶׁלֹּא נִתְכַּוֵּן לְקַבֵּל הַמַּיִם, לְכָךְ לֹא נַעֲשׂוּ שְׁאוּבִים, דִּכְתִיב [וַיִּקְרָא יא] אַךְ מַעְיָן וּבוֹר, מַה מַּעְיָן שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ תְפִיסַת יְדֵי אָדָם, אַף בּוֹר שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ תְפִיסַת יְדֵי אָדָם:


לנגבן – The Aramaic translation of (Genesis 9:13): “the waters began to dry [from the earth],” for they had certainly dried, and specifically to dry them, that we did not intend for them to receive the water, therefore, they (i.e., the water) would not be made “drawn,” as it is written (Leviticus 11:36): “However, a spring or cistern [in which water is collected shall be pure,]” just as a spring does not have the grasp of the hand by a human, even a well/cistern that does not have the grasp of the hand by a human.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:2)


**אִם עוֹנַת גְּשָׁמִים הִיא.** שֶׁנִּתְקַשְּׁרוּ שָׁמַיִם בְּעָבִים וְיָרְדוּ גְשָׁמִים:


אם עונת גשמים היא – that the heavens did not become knotted with clouds and that rain fell.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:3)


**אִם יֶשׁ בּוֹ כִמְעַט מַיִם בַּבּוֹר.** גָּרְסִינַן. וְלֹא גָרְסִינַן אוֹ:


אם יש בו כמעט מים בבור – we have this reading [of "אם"/if], and we do not have the reading of "או"/or.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:4)


**יְשַׁבֵּר.** הַקַּנְקַנִּים וְיִפְּלוּ הַמַּיִם שֶׁבְּתוֹכָן לַבּוֹר, דְּהַמְשָׁכָה הִיא, כֵּיוָן שֶׁמִּתּוֹךְ כֵּלִים שְׁבוּרִים נוֹפְלִים לַבּוֹר:


ישבר – the jars/cannisters [should be broken] and the water that is in them will fall to the cistern/pit. That this is the conducting of the water through a channel, since that within broken utensils, they (i.e., the water) fall to the cistern/pit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:5)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ עוֹנַת גְּשָׁמִים, אִי נַמִּי שֶׁאֵין מַיִם בַּבּוֹר:


ואם לאו – that it is not the rainy season, alternatively, that there is no water in the pit/cistern.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:6)


**לֹא יְשַׁבֵּר.** דְּאִם אֵינָהּ עוֹנַת גְּשָׁמִים הֲרֵי לֹא יוּכַל לַעֲשׂוֹת מִקְוֶה מִן הַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּקַּנְקַנִּים בִּלְבַד, דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר שְׁאוּבָה שֶׁהִמְשִׁיכוּהָ כֻלָּהּ אוֹ רֻבָּהּ, פְּסוּלָה. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהִיא עוֹנַת גְּשָׁמִים, יְשַׁבֵּר לְהַמְשִׁיךְ אֵלּוּ הַמַּיִם וְיִתְמַלֵּא רֹב הַמִּקְוֶה בְּמֵי גְשָׁמִים. וּכְשֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מַיִם כְּלָל, לֹא יְשַׁבֵּר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִיא עוֹנַת גְּשָׁמִים, שֶׁמָּא יִפְּלוּ מִן הַמַּיִם לַבּוֹר רְבִיעִית קֹדֶם שְׁבִירָה. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לְטַעְמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר לְעֵיל רְבִיעִית מַיִם פּוֹסְלִים אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה (לכתחלה) [בַּתְּחִלָּה]:


לא ישבר – for if it is not the rainy season, he would not be able to make a Mikveh/ritual bath from the water that is in the jars/cannisters alone, for Rabbi Eliezer holds that drawn waters, all of which or most of which were conducted through a channel is invalids. But when it is the rainy season, he would break it to channel these waters, and most of the Mikveh would be filled up from rain water. But when there isn’t any water in it (i.e., the Mikveh) at all, he should not break it, even though it is the rainy season, lest there fall from the water a quarter of a LOG into the pit/cistern prior to breaking it. But Rabbi Eliezer, according to his reasoning, who stated above (see Tractate Mikvaot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 4), that a quarter of a LOG of water ab initio invalidates the Mikveh/ritual bath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:7)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ יְשַׁבֵּר.** דְּקָסָבַר שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין פּוֹסְלִין אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה בֵּין בַּתְּחִלָּה בֵּין בַּסּוֹף, וְלֹא רְבִיעִית, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יִפְּלוּ לַבּוֹר שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין קֹדֶם שְׁבִירָה. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלָאו עוֹנַת גְּשָׁמִים הִיא, יְשַׁבֵּר, דְּיָכוֹל לְהַמְשִׁיךְ עַד שִׁעוּר מִקְוֶה, דְּקָסָבַר שְׁאוּבָה שֶׁהִמְשִׁיכָהּ כֻּלָּהּ, טְהוֹרָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ר' יהושע אומר בין כך ובין כך ישבר – that he (i.e., Rabbi Yehoshua) holds that three LOGS [of drawn water] invalidate the Mikveh/ritual bath whether at the beginning or whether at the end, and not one-quarter of a LOG, and we do not suspect that perhaps three LOGS [of drawn water] will fall into the Mikveh/ritual bath prior to breaking [of the jar/cannister]. But even though it is not the rainy season, he should break it, for it is able to continue until [it reaches] the measurement of the Mikveh, for he holds that drawn [water] that continues throughout, is ritually pure. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:8)


**אוֹ יִכְפֶּה.** שֶׁכּוֹפֶה הַקַּנְקַנִּים שֶׁעַל הַגַּג שֶׁלֹּא יִפְּלוּ הַמַּיִם מִתּוֹכָן לַבּוֹר, אֶלָּא יִפְּלוּ לְצַד אַחֵר וּמִשָּׁם נִמְשָׁכִים לַבּוֹר:


או יכפה (or turn it upside down) – that he turns the jars/cannisters that are on the roof upside down so that that the water doesn’t fall from inside them [directly] into the cistern/pit, but rather, that they should fall to one side, and from there continue to the pit/cistern.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:7:9)


**אֲבָל לֹא יְעָרֶה.** שֶׁאִם הִגְבִּיהַּ הַקַּנְקַנִּים וְעֵרָה אוֹתָן, נַעֲשׂוּ כְמַיִם שְׁאוּבִין, שֶׁהֲרֵי יֵשׁ בָּהֶן תְּפִיסַת יְדֵי אָדָם:


אבל לא יערה – that if he raised/lifted up the jars/cannister and emptied them, they (i.e., the waters) would become like drawn water, for they have the grasp of the hand of a human.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:1)


**הַסַּיָּד.** אֻמָּן הַסָּד אֶת הַבּוֹר בְּסִיד כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּחֲזִיק מֵימָיו, וְדַרְכּוֹ לְהָבִיא הַסִּיד לַבּוֹר בְּעָצִיץ, וְאוֹתוֹ בוֹר הָיוּ בוֹ מַיִם כְּשֵׁרִים וְנִשְׁקַע הֶעָצִיץ לְתוֹךְ הַמַּיִם וְנִתְמַלֵּא:


הסייד – an artisan who plasters the pit/cistern with plaster/lime in order that it hold its waters, and his practice was to bring the lime/plaster in a common earthen vessel/pot , and that pit/cistern had kosher water in it but when the common earthen vessel/pot was sunk in the water and became filled up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:2)


**אִם הָיוּ הַמַּיִם צָפִים עַל גַּבָּיו כָּל שֶׁהוּא יְשַׁבֵּר.** שֶׁלֹּא נִשְׁאֲבוּ הַמַּיִם, דְּכָל שָׁעָה הֵן מְעֹרָבִין. אֲבָל אִם הָיָה מַעֲלֶה הֶעָצִיץ בְּיָדוֹ הָיוּ נִפְסָקִים וְנַעֲשִׂים תְּלוּשִׁין:


אם היו המים צפים על גביו כל שהוא ישבר – so that the water not become drawn, for all the while that they are combined/mixed. But if he would raise the common earthen vessel/pot in his hand, they would stop and would become detached.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:3)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁאֵין הַמַּיִם צָפִים עַל גַּבָּיו:


ואם לאו – that the waters did not float on top of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:4)


**לֹא יְשַׁבֵּר.** וּכְשֶׁאֵינָהּ עוֹנַת גְּשָׁמִים אַיְרֵי. דְּאִם הָיָה עוֹנַת גְּשָׁמִים כֵּיוָן דְּאִיכָּא מַיִם בַּבּוֹר יְשַׁבֵּר, כְּדַאֲמַרַן:


לא ישבר – and we are speaking of when it is not the rainy season. For if it was the rainy season, since there is water in the pit/cistern, he should break it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:8:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ יְשַׁבֵּר.** דְּיָכוֹל לְהַמְשִׁיךְ, כִּדְפָרִישְׁנָא לְעֵיל:


רבי יהושע אומר בין כך ובין כך ישבר – for it is able to continue, as we explained above [in Mishnah 7].



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:9:1)


**הַמְסַדֵּר קַנְקַנִּים בַּבּוֹר וְנִתְמַלְּאוּ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבָּלַע הַבּוֹר אֶת מֵימָיו.** וְאֵין מַיִם בַּבּוֹר אֶלָּא אֵלּוּ שֶׁבַּקַּנְקַנִּים:


המסדר קנקנים בבור ונתמלאו אף על פי שבלע הבור את מימיו – but water that is in the cistern/pit is none other than that which are in the jars/cannisters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:9:2)


**הֲרֵי זֶה יְשַׁבֵּר.** כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ דְּאֵין רְבִיעִית פּוֹסֵל, וּמְשַׁבֵּר אֶת הַקַּנְקַנִּים וְעוֹשֶׂה מִקְוֶה בַתְּחִלָּה:


הרי זה ישבר – according to Rabbi Yehoshua that one-quarter [of a LOG of water] invalidates [the Mikveh] and he breaks the jars/canisters and makes a Mikveh at the outset.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר בַּטִּיט וּבַמַּיִם.** כְּשֶׁהַמַּיִם צָפִים עַל גַּבֵּי הַטִּיט וַאֲפִלּוּ רַגְלָיו שׁוֹקְעוֹת בַּטִּיט מַטְבִּילִין לְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַמַּיִם מַקְדִּימִים. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לֵית לֵיהּ הַאי סְבָרָא. אֲבָל אִם הָיָה מַטְבִּיל כְּלִי קָטָן בַּמַּיִם הַצָּפִים עַל גַּבֵּי הַטִּיט, בְּהָא מוֹדֶה רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּעָלְתָה לוֹ טְבִילָה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה שׁוֹקֵעַ בַּטִּיט כְּלָל וּמִצְטָרֵף לְהַשְׁלִים אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


רבי יהושע אומר בטיט ובמים – when the water floats on top of the mud/plaster/clay and even his feet are sunk in the mud/clay/plaster, they immerse objects, according to Rabbi Yehoshua, because the waters came first. But Rabbi Eliezer does not have this line of thought. But if he would immerse a small utensil in the water that floats on top of the mud/clay/plaster, in this Rabbi Eliezer admits that the immersion was acceptable, since it did not sink in the mud/clay at all and [they] combine to complete forty Seah. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:2)


**שֶׁהַקָּנֶה יוֹרֵד מֵאֵלָיו.** שֶׁהוּא רַךְ וְנִמּוֹחַ כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁאִם יָשִׂימוּ בוֹ קָנֶה יִשְׁקַע בַּטִּיט מֵאֵלָיו:


שהקנה יורד מאליו – that it is soft and worn out so much that if they would put in it a reed, it would sink in the mud/clay/plaster on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:3)


**מְקוֹם שֶׁאֵין קְנֵה הַמִּדָּה עוֹמֵד.** קָנֶה שֶׁמּוֹדְדִין בּוֹ אֵינוֹ עוֹמֵד מֵאֵלָיו בְּתוֹכוֹ, וְזֶה אֵינוֹ רַךְ וְנִמּוֹחַ כָּרִאשׁוֹן:


מקום שאין קנה המדה עומד – the rod/reed that they measure with does not stand on its own with it, and this is not soft and worn out like at first.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:4)


**מִשְׁקֹלֶת.** בְּדִיל שֶׁל הַבַּנָּאִים. וְכָל הַשִּׁעוּרִין הַלָּלוּ כָּל אֶחָד מוֹסִיף בְּקֹשִׁי הַטִּיט וְעָבְיוֹ, שֶׁהָרִאשׁוֹן רַךְ מִכֻּלָּם, וְהַשֵּׁנִי קָשֶׁה הֵימֶנּוּ, וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁי קָשֶׁה הֵימֶנּוּ, וְכֵן כֻּלָּם:


משקולת (plummet) – that which refers to builders/masons. And all of these measurements each one adds with the difficulty of the clay/plaster and its thickness, for the first is softer than all of them, and the second is harder than it, and the third is harder than it (i.e., the second), and similarly all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:5)


**בְּפִי חָבִית.** שֶׁפִּיהָ צַר וְקָטָן. וְאִם הַטִּיט רַךְ כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁיָּכוֹל לִשָּׁפֵךְ וּלְהִכָּנֵס בַּפִּי הַצַּר שֶׁל חָבִית, עָלְתָה לוֹ טְבִילָה:


בפי חבית – for its opening is narrow and small. But if the plaster/clay is so soft that it can spill and enter into the narrow mouth of the jar, the immersion has been successful.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:6)


**שְׁפוֹפֶרֶת הַנּוֹד.** שְׁפוֹפֶרֶת שֶׁל קָנֶה שֶׁמַּנִּיחִין עַל פִּי הַנּוֹד שֶׁשּׁוֹפְכִים לְתוֹכוֹ. וְשִׁעוּר הַנֶּקֶב שֶׁל שְׁפוֹפֶרֶת הַנּוֹד, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ חוֹזְרוֹת בּוֹ שְׁתֵּי אֶצְבָּעוֹת בֵּינוֹנִיּוֹת, שֶׁהֵן רֹחַב שְׁתֵּי אֶצְבָּעוֹת הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת שֶׁל פַּס הַיָּד, לֹא הַגּוּדָל:


שפופרת הנוד (the tube/mouthpiece of the leather bottle) – the tube of the reed that they place upon the mouth of the leather bottle when they pour into it. And the measure of the incision/hole of the tube of the reed that they place on the mouthpiece of the leather bottle, that it would return two mid-range fingers, which is the width of the two first fingers of the palm of the hand, but not the thumb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 2:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/2:10:7)


**הַנִּמְדָּד בַּלֹּג.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא רַךְ כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁהוּא נִמְדָּד בַּלֹּג וְהוּא כְלִי שֶׁמַּחֲזִיק שֵׁשׁ בֵּיצִים, הוּא נִמְדָּד עִם הַמִּקְוֶה וְטוֹבְלִין בּוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


הנמדד בלוג – all the time that it so soft that it can be measured by a LOG and this is the utensil that holds six eggs, it is measured with the Mikveh/ritual bath and they immerse in it. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:1)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְלָזֶה לֹג וּמֶחֱצָה וְנִתְעָרְבוּ. וַהֲרֵי יֵשׁ כָּאן שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין שֶׁהֵן פּוֹסְלִין אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה, אַף עַל פִּי כֵן כְּשֵׁרִין:


רבי יוסי אומר. ולזה לוג ומחצה ונתערבו – And behold there are three LOG of drawn water that invalidate the Mikveh, nevertheless, they are kosher/fit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא נִקְרָא עֲלֵיהֶם שֵׁם פְּסוּל.** שֶׁאִם הָיוּ מוֹסִיפִין מַיִם כְּשֵׁרִין בְּכָל אֶחָד עַד תַּשְׁלוּם שִׁעוּר שֶׁל מִקְוֶה, הָיָה כָל אֶחָד מֵהֶן כָּשֵׁר:


שלא נקרא עלהים שם פסול – that if they add kosher water (i.e., rainwater, for example) in all of them until the completion of the measurement of the Mikveh, that each one of them was kosher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:3)


**וְנֶחֱלַק לִשְׁנַיִם.** וְהוֹסִיפוּ בְכָל אֶחָד מַיִם כְּשֵׁרִים עַד שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּכָל אֶחָד מֵהֶם כְּשִׁעוּר מִקְוֶה:


ונחלק לשנים – and they added to each one of them kosher water until there is in each one of them according to the measurement of the Mikveh (i.e., forty Seah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:4)


**פָּסוּל.** כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶן:


פסול – each one of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:5)


**וְחָסַר אֲפִלּוּ קֹרְטוֹב.** לְאַחַר שֶׁנָּפְלוּ עָלָיו שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין מְצֻמְצָמִים נֶחְסַר מִמֶּנּוּ אֲפִלּוּ קֹרְטוֹב שֶׁל מַיִם:


וחסר אפילו קרטוב – after three contracted/squeezed in LOGS of drawn water fell in it that was missing even a Kartuv (i.e., a small liquid measure equivalent to 1/64th of a LOG) of water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:6)


**כָּשֵׁר.** לְהוֹסִיף עָלָיו מַיִם וּלְהַשְׁלִימוֹ לְשִׁעוּר מִקְוֶה. שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁבְּאוֹתוֹ קֹרְטוֹב שֶׁחָסַר, לֹא חָסֵר מְעַט מִן הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין:


כשר – [it is kosher/fit] to add upon it water to complete it for the measurement of a Mikveh/ritual bath. It is impossible that with this one that is lacking is not missing a little bit from the three LOGS, and it is found that there weren’t three LOGS of drawn water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:7)


**קֹרְטוֹב.** חֵלֶק מִשִּׁשִּׁים בַּלֹּג:


קרטוב – 1/64th of a LOG.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:8)


**עַד שֶׁיֵּצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ מְלֹאוֹ.** הַמַּיִם שֶׁהָיוּ בוֹ תְחִלָּה כְּשֶׁנָּפְלוּ בוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין:


עד שיצא ממנו מלואו – that water that was in it initially when the three LOGS of drawn water fell in.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:1:9)


**וָעוֹד.** מְעַט יוֹתֵר כְּדֵי לְחַסֵּר מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין. כְּגוֹן מִקְוֶה שֶׁהָיוּ בוֹ עֶשְׂרִים סְאָה מַיִם כְּשֵׁרִים וְנָפְלוּ לְתוֹכוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין וְנִתְמַלֵּא אַחַר כָּךְ מֵי גְשָׁמִים, לְעוֹלָם הוּא בִפְסוּלוֹ עַד שֶׁיּוֹצִיא מִמֶּנּוּ עֶשְׂרִים סְאָה שֶׁהָיוּ שָׁם תְּחִלָּה, וְעוֹד מְעַט יוֹתֵר, לְמַעֵט שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין:


ועוד –a bit more in order to omit/lessen from the three LOGS [of drawn water]. As for example, a Mikveh which had in it twenty Seah of kosher water and three LOGS of drawn water fell into it and it was filled up afterwards with rain water, it will always be in its status of invalidity/unfitness until he removes from it the twenty Seah that were there initially, and a little bit more, to omit three LOGS [of drawn water].



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:2:1)


**שֶׁיַּעֲמִיד בֶּחָצֵר אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה.** מִקְוֶה כָשֵׁר לְמַטָּה מִן הַפָּסוּל, וּפוֹתְקָן לַכָּשֵׁר כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְחַבְּרוּ, וְיִטְהַר הָעֶלְיוֹן הַפָּסוּל מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹן הַכָּשֵׁר, דְּאָמְרִינַן גּוּד אַסֵּיק. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן אִם הַכָּשֵׁר לְמַעְלָה וְהַפָּסוּל לְמַטָּה, דְּאָמְרִינַן גּוּד אַחֵית:


שיעמיד בחצר ארבעים סאה – a kosher Mikveh below from the invalid/unfit one, and he opens a channel to the Kosher one (i.e., Mikveh) so that they can combine and the upper unit/invalid one can be purified from the lower Kosher one (i.e., Mikveh), as we stated, we raise/stretch it (i.e., the partitions – adopting the legal fictions that the partitions around the stand are prolonged). And all the more so, if the kosher [Mikveh] is above and the unfit/invalid one is below, for we state that we stretch and bring the partitions down (i.e., adopt the fiction that the walls are prolonged so as to reach the bottom).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:2:2)


**אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן פָּסָק.** שֶׁפָּסְקוּ מֵימָיו וְיָצְאוּ מִמֶּנּוּ מְלוֹאוֹ וְעוֹד כְּדַאֲמַרַן. וְיֵשׁ גּוֹרְסִים אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן פָּקָק, כְּלוֹמַר אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן סָתַם עַל הַמִּקְוֶה אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה עַד שֶׁלֹּא יִגַּע אֶחָד בְּאֵלֶּה הַמַּיִם. וְגִרְסָא רִאשׁוֹנָה נִרְאֶה לִי עִקָּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה:


אא"כ פסק – that the waters spotted and went out from it to its fullness and a bit more, as we stated (in Mishnah 1). And there are those who have the reading [at the end of the Mishnah]: "אא"כ פקק"/unless he inserted a stop-gap/stopped it up, meaning to say, unless he closed off on the Mikveh that he had made until it will not make contact/touch any of the water. But the first reading appears to me to be the essential one. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eleazar ben Azariah.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:1)


**וְהָאַמָּה נִכְנֶסֶת לוֹ.** כְּגוֹן מֵי גְשָׁמִים שֶׁבָּאוּ מִן הַמִּדְרוֹן וְנִכְנָסִים לוֹ:


האמה נכנסת לו (the water channel enters it) – as for example rain waters that come from the sloping and enter it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:2)


**הַסּוֹחֵט אֶת כְּסוּתוֹ.** וְהַמַּיִם שֶׁהָיוּ נִבְלָעִים בּוֹ חֲשִׁיבֵי מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים, וּכְשֶׁהוּא סוֹחֲטָן לְתוֹךְ הַבּוֹר הֵן בָּאִין מִמְּקוֹמוֹת הַרְבֵּה שֶׁמִּכָּאן וּמִכָּאן לַבֶּגֶד:


הסוחט את כסותו (a person who wrings out his garment) – and the water that was absorbed by it is considered drawn water, and when he wrings them out into the cistern/pit they come from many places, that from here and from there to the clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:3)


**וְהַמְּעָרֶה מִן הַצַּרְצוּר.** כְּלִי חֶרֶס שֶׁעוֹשִׂין עַל פִּיו שְׂבָכָה כְּמִין מִכְבָּר מַעֲשֵׂה רֶשֶׁת, וְהַמְעָרֶה מִמֶּנּוּ מַטִּיל הַמַּיִם מִמְּקוֹמוֹת הַרְבֵּה:


והמערה מן הצרצור (if one pouring water out of a cooler throws water out in several places) – an earthenware utensil that they make on its mouth network of lattice-work, and when he empties [water] from it, he pours in water from many places.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:4)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מַכְשִׁיר.** קָסָבַר דְּאֵין שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּים מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין פּוֹסְלִין אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן מַטִּילָן לַמִּקְוֶה מִכְּלִי אֶחָד וּמִמָּקוֹם אֶחָד, מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בִּכְסוּת וְצַרְצוּר:


ר' עקיבא מכשיר – for he (i.e., Rabbi Akiva) holds that three LOGS of drawn water do not invalidate the Mikveh/ritual bath unless he pours them from one utensil and from one place, what is not the case with garments and a water cooler.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים פּוֹסְלִים.** דְּסָבְרֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה כֵלִים מִצְטָרְפִין:


וחכמים פוסלים – for they (i.e., the Sages) hold that three utensils combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:6)


**לֹא אָמְרוּ מַטִּילִין אֶלָּא מַטִּיל.** כָּל זֶה קִבֵּל רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מֵרַבּוֹתָיו, דִּכְלִי הַמַּטִּיל שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם לַמִּקְוֶה פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה:


לא אמרו מטילין אלא מטיל – according to this language, Rabbi Akiva received [the tradition] from his Rabbis/teachers, a utensil that pours three LOGS of water to a Mikveh invalidates the Mikveh/ritual bath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:3:7)


**וְלֹא כָךְ אָמְרוּ אֶלָּא שֶׁנָּפְלוּ.** וְלֹא שְׁנָא מִכְּלִי אֶחָד אוֹ מִכֵּלִים הַרְבֵּה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


ולא כך אמרו אלא שנפלו – it does not make a difference whether it is from one utensil or from many utensils. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:1)


**מִכְּלִי אֶחָד מִשְּׁנַיִם וְכוּ'.** סִיּוּמָא דְמִלְּתַיְהוּ דְרַבָּנָן הִיא:


מכלי אחד משנים וכו' – the conclusion of their matter is according to the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:2)


**תִּשְׁעָה קַבִּין מַיִם.** בַּעַל קֶרִי חוֹלֶה שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִטְבֹּל, אִם נָתְנוּ עָלָיו תִּשְׁעָה קַבִּין מַיִם, טָהוֹר. וְאַשְׁמְעִינַן מַתְנִיתִין דְּלֹא שְׁנָא מִכְּלִי אֶחָד לֹא שְׁנָא מִשְּׁנַיִם אוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה כֵלִים:


תשעה קבין מים – one who experienced a seminal emission (i.e., whether involuntarily, intentionally or during sexual relations, he becomes ritually impure – and must immerse himself in a ritual bath, and after nightfall on the day of his immersion, becomes ritually pure) who is infirm and is not able to immerse [in a Mikveh], if they placed upon him nine KABS (i.e., a KAB = one-sixth of a SEAH, or twenty- four egg bulks) of water, he is ritually pure. And our Mishnah comes to teach us that it doesn’t matter whether one uses one utensil, two utensils or three utensils.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:3)


**וְכֵן טָהוֹר שֶׁנָּפְלוּ עַל רֹאשׁוֹ וְעַל רֻבּוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין.** דְּפוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן פֶּרֶק קַמָּא דְשַׁבָּת [דַּף יג], הָנֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מִשְּׁנַיִם אוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה כֵלִים מִצְטָרְפִין, מֵאַרְבָּעָה אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין:


וכן טהור שנפלו על ראשו ועל רובו שלשת לוגין מים שאובין – that invalidate the priest-due/heave-offering, as we stated in the first chapter of [Tractate] Shabbat [13b – and Tractate Zavim, Chapter 5, Mishnah 12]. These three LOGS, from two or three utensils – combine; from four [utensils] they do not combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:4)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים.** דְּמִשְּׁנַיִם וּשְׁלֹשָׁה כֵלִים מִצְטָרְפִין:


במה דברים אמורים – that from two or three utensils they combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:5)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁלֹּא נִתְכַּוֵּן לְרַבּוֹת.** אַמִּקְוֶה קָאֵי, שֶׁלֹּא נִתְכַּוֵּן לְהוֹסִיף עָלָיו כְּדֵי לְהַרְבּוֹת בּוֹ מַיִם:


בזמן שלא נתכוין לרבות (at the time that he did not intend to add to more [drawn] water) – it is referring to the Mikveh, that he didn’t intend to add to it in order to increase its water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 3:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/3:4:6)


**אֲפִלּוּ קֹרְטוֹב.** שִׁעוּר מֻעָט, אֶחָד מִשִּׁשִּׁים וְאַרְבָּעָה בַּלֹּג:


אפילו קרטוב – a small measurement, one sixth-fourth of a LOG.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:1)


**הַמַּנִּיחַ כֵּלִים תַּחַת הַצִּנּוֹר.** מַרְזֵב שֶׁמֵּי גְשָׁמִים נִכְנָסִים בּוֹ וְיוֹרְדִים דֶּרֶךְ פִּיו לַמִּקְוֶה. וְהָכָא מַיְרֵי בְצִנּוֹר שֶׁל עֵץ שֶׁקְּבָעוֹ וּלְבַסּוֹף חֲקָקוֹ, דְּהוֹאִיל וְלֹא הָיָה תוֹרַת כְּלִי עָלָיו כְּשֶׁקְּבָעוֹ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחֲקָקוֹ אַחֲרֵי כֵן לָא חֲשִׁיבֵי הַמַּיִם שֶׁבְּתוֹכוֹ מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים, הוֹאִיל וְלֹא הָיָה תוֹרַת כְּלִי עָלָיו בְּתָלוּשׁ:


המניח כלים תחת הצנור (one who puts vessels under the spout – which feeds a ritual bath) – a tube/spout (or a movable tube attached to the roof gutters) that rain waters enter through and descend through its mouth to the Mikveh/ritual bath. But here we are speaking of a wooden spout that was established and eventually hollowed out, for when it was established it did not have the law of a utensil upon it when it was detached.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:2)


**וְאֶחָד כֵּלִים קְטַנִּים.** דְּלֹא תֵימָא דְלָא חֲשִׁיבֵי:


ואחד כלים קטנים – that you should not say that they were not important (see also Tractate Kelim, Chapter 15, Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:3)


**אֲפִלּוּ כְלֵי גְלָלִים.** דְּלָא חֲשִׁיבֵי כֵלִים לְעִנְיַן טֻמְאָה, חֲשִׁיבֵי כֵלִים לִפְסֹל אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה, אֲפִלּוּ אִם שְׁבָרָם אוֹ כָפָה אוֹתָם, דְּלֹא דָמֵי לְמַנִּיחַ קַנְקַנִּים בְּרֹאשׁ הַגַּג לְנַגְּבָן:


אפילו כלי גללים – while they are not considered utensils with regard to ritual impurity, they are considered utensils to invalidate the Mikveh/ritual bath, even if he broke them or emptied them, for it is not similar to leaving jars/cannisters at the top of the roof to dry them out (see Tractate Mikvaot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 7).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:4)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל מְטַהֲרִין בְּשׁוֹכֵחַ.** בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דְשַׁבָּת בַּגְּמָרָא, מוֹקְמֵי פְלוּגְתַּיְהוּ כְּשֶׁהִנִּיחָן תַּחַת הַצִּנּוֹר בִּשְׁעַת קִשּׁוּר עָבִים וְנִתְפַּזְּרוּ וּשְׁכָחָן וְחָזְרוּ וְנִתְקַשְּׁרוּ, דְּבֵית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי מִשּׁוּם פִּזּוּר עָבִים לֹא נִתְבַּטְּלָה מַחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ הָרִאשׁוֹנָה, דְּגַלֵּי דַעְתֵּיהּ שֶׁרָצָה שֶׁיִּפְּלוּ לְתוֹכָן. וּבֵית הִלֵּל סָבְרֵי בָּטְלָה מַחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ, דְּכִי נִתְפַּזְּרוּ, אַסַּח דַּעְתֵּיהּ, דְּקָסָבַר לֹא יֵרְדוּ גְשָׁמִים עוֹד. אֲבָל הִנִּיחָן תַּחַת הַצִּנּוֹר בִּשְׁעַת קִשּׁוּר עָבִים וְאֵחֲרוּ הַגְּשָׁמִים לָבֹא וְיָצָא לִמְלַאכְתּוֹ וּשְׁכָחָן, דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל הוֹאִיל וּמִתְּחִלָּה לְכָךְ נִתְכַּוֵּן לֹא בָטְלָה מַחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ בְשִׁכְחָתוֹ. וְאִם הִנִּיחָן בִּשְׁעַת פִּזּוּר עָבִים, דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל טְהוֹרִים, שֶׁלֹּא נִתְכַּוֵּן מִתְּחִלָּה לְכָךְ:


ובית הלל מטהרין בשוכח – In the first chapter of Tractate Shabbat in the Gemara (16b), their dispute is established (i.e., between the Schools of Hillel and Shammai) concerning when he left them (i.e., utensils) under the spout at the time when the clouds were gathering and then they dispersed and he forgot them (i.e., the leaving of the utensils under the spout) and then the clouds gathered once again, that the School of Shammai holds that because of the dispersal of the clouds his first intention/thought process was not nullified, for he revealed his intention that he wanted that they (i.e., rain waters) would fall into them. But the School of Hillel holds that his intention/thought process was nullified, for when the clouds dispersed, his attention was diverted, for he held that rain would not fall any longer. But when he left them (i.e., the utensils) underneath the spout at the time of the gathering of the clouds, and the rains were late to come, and he went off to his work and forgot them, everyone holds that since ab initio, it was for this that he intended, his intention/thought process was not nullified through his forgetting. But if he left them (i.e., the utensils) at the time when the clouds dispersed, everyone holds that they are ritually pure, for he did not intend ab initio for this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:5)


**בְּשׁוֹכֵחַ בֶּחָצֵר שֶׁהוּא טָהוֹר.** בֶּחָצֵר שֶׁלֹּא תַחַת הַצִּנּוֹר, וְנִתְמַלְּאוּ מִן הַנּוֹטְפִין וְנָפְלוּ לַמִּקְוֶה. דְּדַוְקָא תַחַת הַצִּנּוֹר הוּא דִפְלִיגֵי בֵית שַׁמַּאי, דְּגַלֵּי דַעְתֵּיהּ דְּרָצָה שֶׁיִּפְּלוּ לְתוֹכָן, וּמִשּׁוּם פִּזּוּר לֹא בָטְלָה מַחֲשָׁבָה קַמַּיְתָא. אֲבָל מַנִּיחַ בֶּחָצֵר, אֲפִלּוּ בִשְׁעַת קִשּׁוּר, לֹא מוּכְחָא מֵעִקָּרָא מַחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ קַמַּיְתָא שַׁפִּיר, הִלְכָּךְ בָּטְלָה כְשֶׁנִּתְפַּזְּרוּ:


בשכח בחצר שהוא טהור – underneath the spout and it was filled from the dripping rain water and fell into the Mikveh/ritual bath. For specifically underneath the spout is where the School of Shammai disagrees, for he reveals his intention that he wanted that [the rain waters] would fall into them, but because of the dispersal [of the clouds], the first thought was not nullified. But if he leaves it in the courtyard [but not under the spout], even at the time when the gathering [of the clouds], it was not proven ab initio that his first pattern of thinking/intention was best, therefore, it (i.e., his thought) was nullified when they (i.e., the clouds) dispersed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:1:6)


**עֲדַיִן מַחֲלֹקֶת בִּמְקוֹמָהּ עוֹמָדֶת.** שֶׁלֹּא עָמְדוּ לְמִנְיָן עַל כָּךְ וְלֹא רַבּוּ תַלְמִידֵי בֵית שַׁמַּאי:


עדיין מחלוקת במקומה עומדת – it did not stand for a vote and the students of the School of Shammai were not greater [than those of the School of Hillel].



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:1)


**הַמַּנִּיחַ טַבְלָא תַּחַת הַצִּנּוֹר.** לְהַמְשִׁיךְ הַמַּיִם לַמִּקְוֶה:


המניח טבלא תחת הצנור (he who leaves a tray/board underneath the spout) – to conduct the water into the Mikveh/ritual bath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:2)


**אִם יֶשׁ לָהּ לְבִזְבֵּז.** שָׂפָה סָבִיב מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹתֶיהָ, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לָהּ בֵּית קִבּוּל:


אם יש לה לבזבז (if it has a vertical rim/edge – by which a flat utensil is made into a vessel-lie receptacle) – a rim around from the four directions in order that it will be for it a receptacle).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:3)


**פּוֹסֶלֶת אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה.** דַּהֲווּ לְהוּ מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים. וּמִשּׁוּם צִנּוֹר לֹא הָיָה מִפְּסִיל מִקְוֶה, דְּאַיְרֵי בְצִנּוֹר שֶׁקְּבָעוֹ וּלְבַסּוֹף חֲקָקוֹ כְּדִפְרִישִׁית:


פוסלת את המקוה – it would be for them drawn water, and because the spout itself does not invalidate the Mikveh, we are speaking of a spout that was established [first] and at the end, they hollow it/shape it into a receptacle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:4)


**זְקָפָהּ לִדּוֹחַ.** כְּדֵי שֶׁתּוּדַח מִטִּנֹּפֶת שֶׁבָּהּ:


זקפה לידוח (if he set it upright to rinse it) – in order that it should be rinsed from the filth that is in it,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:2:5)


**אֵינָהּ פּוֹסֶלֶת אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה.** דְּלֹא קַיְמָא כְדֶרֶךְ קַבָּלָתָהּ:


it does not invalidate the Mikveh/ritual bath, for it doesn’t exist in the manner of its reception.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:1)


**הַחוֹטֵט בְּצִנּוֹר.** דֶּרֶךְ צִנּוֹר לִהְיוֹת פָּתוּחַ מִשְּׁנֵי צְדָדִים, וְאֵין פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן חָקַק בּוֹ חֲקָק מְעַט כְּדֵי לְקַבֵּל צְרוֹרוֹת הַמְעַכְּבִים הִלּוּךְ הַמַּיִם, וּכְשֶׁחֲקָקוֹ וּלְבַסּוֹף קְבָעוֹ אַיְרֵי, הוֹאִיל וְהָיָה עָלָיו תּוֹרַת כְּלִי כְשֶׁהָיָה תָלוּשׁ, הֲרֵי הַמַּיִם הָעוֹבְרִים בּוֹ שְׁאוּבִים הֵן וּפוֹסְלִים אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה:


החוטט בצנור (if one makes a cavity in a water pipe – for the deposit of pebbles) – it is the manner of a water pipe to be open from two sides, and this does not invalidate the Mikveh/ritual bath unless he hollowed it out a small cavity in order to receive stones that prevent the passage of the water, and it (i.e., the Mishnah) is speaking of when he hallowed it out and at the end established it/made it permanent, for since, it had upon it the status of a utensil when it was detached, for the water passing through it was drawn [water] and invalidates the Mikveh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:2)


**וּבְשֶׁל חֶרֶס.** לָא חֲשִׁיב כְּלִי אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יֵשׁ לוֹ בֵית קִבּוּל שֶׁל רְבִיעִית:


ובשל חרס – it is not considered a utensil until it has a receptacle of one-quarter [of a LOG].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:3)


**לֹא אָמְרוּ רְבִיעִית אֶלָּא בְשִׁבְרֵי הַחֶרֶס.** דַּוְקָא בִכְלִי חֶרֶס לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּשְׁבַּר בָּעִינַן שֶׁיְּהֵא הַשֶּׁבֶר מַחֲזִיק רְבִיעִית. אֲבָל מִתְּחִלָּתוֹ אֲפִלּוּ כָל שֶׁהוּא חָשׁוּב כְּלִי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


לא אמרו רביעית אלא בשברי החרס – specifically with an earthenware utensil after it was broken we require that he broken piece holds one-quarter [of a LOG], but from its outset, even a little bit/anything is considered a utensil, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:4)


**מִתְחַלְחֲלִים.** מִתְגַּלְגְּלִים בְּתוֹךְ בֵּית קִבּוּל, שֶׁאֵינָם מְהֻדָּקִים בּוֹ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּתְמַלֵּא מֵהֶם אֶלָּא מִתְנַעְנְעִים אֵילָךְ וָאֵילָךְ. לְשׁוֹן וַתִּתְחַלְחַל הַמַּלְכָּה (אסתר ד):


מתחלחלים (thrown about) – [the stones/pebbles] are rolling about within the receptable, and they are not crushed/ground in it even though that it was filled with them, but rather they move about here and there. It is the language of (Esther 4:4) “And the queen was greatly agitated.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:5)


**פּוֹסְלִין אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה.** דְּלֹא נִתְבַּטֵּל קִבּוּלוֹ:


פוסלין את המקוה – for its receiving was not invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:6)


**יָרַד לְתוֹכוֹ.** לְתוֹךְ בֵּית קִבּוּלוֹ:


ירד לתוכו – into the receptacle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:7)


**עָפָר וְנִכְבַּשׁ.** שֶׁהֶעָפָר נִקְבַּע וְנַעֲשָׂה מְהֻדָּק יָפֶה וְהַמַּיִם עוֹבְרִים עָלָיו. תַּרְגּוּם בַּמְּסִלָּה נַעֲלֶה (במדבר כ) בְּאֹרַח כְּבִישָׁא נִסַּק:


עפר ונכבש (dirt and it was pressed tight) – the dirt was settled and made nicely fastened and the water passes over it. The [Aramaic] Targum of (Numbers 20:19): “We will keep to the beaten track,” – we will go up on the paved path.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:8)


**כָּשֵׁר.** דְּאֵין כָּאן בֵּית קִבּוּל:


כשר – for there isn’t a receptacle here.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:9)


**סִלּוֹן וְצִנּוֹר וּמַרְזֵב.** אֶחָד הוּא:


סילון (pipe/duct/tube) -and a water spout and tube/spout, are one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 4:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/4:3:10)


**שֶׁהוּא צַר מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן.** מִשְּׁנֵי פִיּוֹתָיו, מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁהַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִים בּוֹ וּמִמָּקוֹם שֶׁיּוֹרְדִים מִמֶּנּוּ לַמִּקְוֶה:


שהוא צר מכאן ומכאן – from its two mouths, from the place where the water enters into it and from the place that it does down from it into the Mikveh.
