**אַף הַשֵּׁנִי טָהוֹר.** דְּאָמְרִינַן גּוּד אַחֵית, וְהָוֵי כְאִלּוּ הַמַּיִם שֶׁהֶעֱלָה הָרִאשׁוֹן בְּגוּפוֹ הֵן מְחֻבָּרִין לְמֵי מִקְוֶה וְלֹא נֶחְסַר מִשִּׁעוּרוֹ כְלוּם. וּפֵרְשׁוּ בַגְּמָרָא דַחֲגִיגָה [דַּף יט] דְּלֹא טִהֵר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אֶלָּא בְמַעֲלוֹת דְּרַבָּנָן, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה טָהוֹר לְחֻלִּין וְטָבַל לִהְיוֹת טָהוֹר לְמַעֲשֵׂר, אוֹ שֶׁהָיָה טָהוֹר לְמַעֲשֵׂר וְטָבַל לִהְיוֹת טָהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה. אֲבָל לַעֲלוֹת מִטֻּמְאָה גְמוּרָה לְטָהֳרָה, דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל טָמֵא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אף השני טהור – for we say that we lower [the partition], for it is as if the water that the first person brought up on his body is attached to the waters of the Mikveh, and it (i.e., the Mikveh) is not lacking anything from its measurement. And they explained in the Gemara (i.e., Talmud) of [Tractate] Hagigah [19a] that Rabbi Yehuda did not purify other than according to the gradations of the Rabbis, as for example that he was ritually pure for non-sacred things and he immersed in order to become ritually pure for tithes, or that he was ritually pure for tithes and he immersed in order to become pure for priest’s due/heave offerings. But to ascend from complete ritual impurity to purity, he is ritually impure to everyone. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:6:3)


**סָגוֹס.** בֶּגֶד צֶמֶר עָב, וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בַעֲרָבִי אלבורנו"ס, וּבוֹלֵעַ מַיִם הַרְבֵּה:


סגוס (coarse woolen blanket – usually as a mattress to sleep on) – a thick woolen clothing, and we call it in Arabic ALBORNOS, and it absorbs a lot of water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:6:4)


**מִקְצָתוֹ נוֹגֵעַ בַּמַּיִם טָהוֹר.** וּבְמִקְוֶה שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה מְצֻמְצָמוֹת אַיְרֵי, וְטָבַל בּוֹ אָדָם לְאַחַר שֶׁהִטְבִּיל בּוֹ אֶת הַסָּגוֹס, טָהוֹר הָאִישׁ הַטּוֹבֵל אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנֶּחְסַר שִׁעוּר מִקְוֶה בִּטְבִילַת הַסָּגוֹס, מֵאַחַר שֶׁמִּקְצָת הַסָּגוֹס נוֹגֵעַ בַּמַּיִם. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה הִיא דִסְבִירָא לֵיהּ אָמְרִינַן גּוּד אַחֵית:


מקצתו נוגע במים טהור – and we are speaking of a Mikveh that has in it exactly forty Seah [of water] and another person immersed after he immersed in it the thick woolen cloth, the person who immersed is ritually pure even though that it is lacking the measurement of the Mikveh with the immersion of the thick woolen cloth, for since part of the thick woolen cloth touches the water. And Rabbi Yehuda is the one who holds that we state that גוד אחית/we lower the partition (i.e., it is as if the water on the thick woolen cloth was still in the water).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:6:5)


**הַמַּיִם שֶׁבְּתוֹכָן שְׁאוּבִים.** וְחוֹזְרִים וּפוֹסְלִים אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה בִּשְׁלשָׁה לֻגִּים. שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא הָיוּ בוֹ אֶלָּא אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה מְכֻוָּנוֹת וְנִתְחַסֵּר כְּשֶׁהִגְבִּיהַּ שִׂפְתוֹתֵיהֶן מִן הַמַּיִם:


המים שבתוכו שאובים – and we return and invalidate the Mikveh with the three LOG [of drawn water] for there were only exactly forty Seah [of fit/kosher water] in it and it became lacking when he lifted their lips out of the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:6:6)


**וּמַעֲלֶה אוֹתָם דֶּרֶךְ שׁוּלֵיהֶם.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִפְּלוּ הַמַּיִם שֶׁבְּתוֹכָן לַמִּקְוֶה וְיִפָּסְלוּ כָל מֵימָיו:


ומעלה אותם דרך שוליהם – in order that the water that is within them doesn’t fall to the Mikveh and will invalidate all of its waters.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:1)


**הִטְבִּיל בּוֹ אֶת הַמִּטָּה.** שֶׁרַגְלֶיהָ גְבוֹהוֹת, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהַטְבִּילָהּ כֻּלָּהּ כְּאַחַת בְּמִקְוֶה קָטָן כָּזֶה שֶׁשִּׁעוּרוֹ מְצֻמְצָם, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן רַגְלֶיהָ שׁוֹקְעוֹת בַּטִּיט:


הטביל בו את המטה – whose legs are high, and it is impossible to ritually immerse all of it as one in a small Mikveh like this whose measurements are exact, unless its legs sink down in the [thick] mud.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:2)


**הֶעָבֶה.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ נָרוֹק וְאֵין מַטְבִּילִין בּוֹ:


העבה – that is not miry (see Mishnah 1 of this chapter), and we don’t ritually immerse in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:3)


**שֶׁהַמַּיִם מְקַדְּמִין.** לְהַטְבִּיל הָרַגְלַיִם קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּשְׁקְעוּ בַטִּיט, וּבַמַּיִם הֻטְבְּלוּ:


שהמים מקדמין – to ritually immerse the legs prior to its sinking in the mud, and it was [ritually] immersed in water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:4)


**שֶׁמֵּימָיו מְרֻדָּדִים.** שֶׁאֵין הַמַּיִם עֲמֻקִּים, מֵחֲמַת שֶׁהַמִּקְוֶה רָחָב וְהַמַּיִם מִתְפַּשְּׁטִים בְּכֻלּוֹ. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה, אֵין כָּל גּוּפוֹ מִתְכַּסֶּה בַמַּיִם בְּבַת אַחַת:


שמימיו מרודדים – that the waters are not deep, on account that the Mikveh is wide and the waters spread out throughout all of it, and even though it has forty Seah one’s whole body is not covered with water all at once.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:5)


**כּוֹבֵשׁ.** לְצַד אֶחָד שֶׁל מִקְוֶה:


כובש (presses down) – to one side of the Mikveh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:6)


**אֲפִלּוּ חֲבִילֵי עֵצִים וְקָנִים.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּנִרְאֶה כְמִקְוֶה שֶׁחִלְּקוֹ, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי הוֹאִיל וְהַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִין בֵּינֵיהֶן, לֹא הָוֵי חִלּוּק. וְכוֹבֵשׁ דְּנָקַט, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהָעֵצִים וְהַקָּנִים צָפִים עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם, וְצָרִיךְ לִכְבֹּשׁ עֲלֵיהֶם אֲבָנִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּכָּנְסוּ תַחַת הַמַּיִם:


אפילו חבילי עצים וקנים – and even though it appears like a Mikveh that they divided, nevertheless since the water enters between them, it is not a division. And the word וכובש"”/he presses that it (i.e., the Mishnah) uses, because the wood and the reeds float over the face of the water and heneeds to press upon them stones in order that they will enter underneath the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:7)


**הָיָה מוֹלִיךְ וּמֵבִיא בַמַּיִם.** מְנַעְנֵעַ הַמַּיִם בְּיָדָיו:


היה מוליך ומביא במים – he shakes/stirs the water with his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 7:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/7:7:8)


**כֵּיוָן שֶׁעָבַר הַגַּל.** שֶׁל מַיִם עַל הַמַּעֲלָה שֶׁל מִקְוֶה שֶׁהַמַּחַט מֻנָּח בָּהּ וְצָפוּ מֵי הַגַּל עַל הַמַּחַט, טְהוֹרָה. וּלְפִי שֶׁהַמַּחַט דַּקָּה וּקְטַנָּה וְיָרֵא פֶן תִּפֹּל בַּמַּיִם, דֶּרֶךְ לְהַטְבִּילָהּ כֵּן:


כיון שעבר הגל – [the wave] of water over the top of the Mikveh that the needle is placed in it and the waters of the wave have floated over the needle, it is [ritually] pure. And because the needle is thin and small, and he fears lest it will fall into the water, this is the manner of [ritually] immersing it.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:1)


**אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל טְהוֹרָה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ עֲיָרוֹת שֶׁל כּוּתִים שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ. שֶׁלֹּא גָזְרוּ טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא עַל אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים בְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ:


ארץ ישראל טהורה – and even the villages of the Cutheans (i.e., Samaritans) upon whom they (i.e., the Rabbis) did not decree [ritual] impurity, other than the lands of the nations outside of the Land [of Israel].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:2)


**וּמִקְוְאוֹתֶיהָ טְהוֹרִים.** וְלֹא מַחְזְקִינַן לְהוּ בִשְׁאוּבִים, דִּסְתָמָא בְהֶכְשֵׁר נַעֲשׂוּ:


ומקואותיה טהוהים – and we don’t presume them to be of drawn [water], for undefined they were made kosher/fit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:3)


**לְבַעֲלֵי קְרָיִין.** לְהַתִּירָן בְּדִבְרֵי תוֹרָה. דְּבַעַל קֶרִי אָסוּר בְּדִבְרֵי תוֹרָה עַד שֶׁיִּטְבֹּל, וְכִי טָבֵיל אֲפִלּוּ בְמִקְוֶה שֶׁכָּל מֵימָיו שְׁאוּבִין מֻתָּר:


לבעלי קריין – to permit them by the words of the Torah, for a person who experienced a seminal emission is prohibited by the words of the Torah until he [ritually] immerses, and if he [ritually] immerses in a place where all of its waters are drawn, it is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:4)


**אֲפִלּוּ נִתְמַלְּאוּ בְקִילוֹן.** שֶׁמִּלְּאוּ מַיִם בַּכָּתֵף וְשָׁפְכוּ לְתוֹךְ גֻּמָּא, וּמֵאוֹתָהּ גֻּמָּא יֵשׁ דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁהוֹלְכִים הַמַּיִם לַמִּקְוֶה:


אפילו נתמלאו בקילון (even if they were filled up with a swipe and bucket for drawing water – see also Tractate Moed Katan, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1) – that they filled water with a shoulder and poured it into a hole and from that hole/indentation there is a path that the water travels to the Mikveh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:5)


**שֶׁחוּץ לַמַּפְתֵּחַ.** חוּץ לְפֶתַח הַמְּדִינָה:


שחוץ למפתח – outside of the opening of the town gate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:6)


**כְּשֵׁרִים אַף לְנִדּוֹת.** לְהַתִּירָן לְבַעֲלֵיהֶן, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאִיכָּא אִסּוּר כָּרֵת. וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא חֲסֵרִים הָיוּ וְנָפְלוּ עֲלֵיהֶם מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים וְנִתְמַלְּאוּ, דְּחוּץ לְפֶתַח הַמְּדִינָה אֵין רֶגֶל בְּנֵי אָדָם מְצוּיִין שָׁם:


כשרים אף לנדות – to permit them to their husbands, and even though there is the prohibition of extirpation [for not following the laws of the menstruant woman properly]. But we are not concerned lest they (i.e., the Mikvaot/ritual baths) were lacking [enough water] and that drawn water fell upon them and they were filled up, for outside of the town gate, the foot of people are not found there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:7)


**לִפְנִים מִן הַמַּפְתֵּחַ.** לִפְנִים מִפֶּתַח הַמְּדִינָה:


לפנים מן הפחת – inside from the entrance to the town gate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:8)


**וּפְסוּלִים לְכָל הַטְּמֵאִים.** שֶׁבְּנֵי אָדָם מְצוּיִין שָׁם הַרְבֵּה וּמְמַלְּאִים בַּכָּתֵף מַיִם וְנוֹתְנִים שָׁם לְכַבֵּס וְלִרְחֹץ:


ופסולים לכל הטמאים – for many people are found there and fill up water with their shoulders and give them there [clothing] to launder and to wash.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:9)


**מִפְּנֵי הַכְּבִיסָה.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹמַר חֲסֵרִים הָיוּ וְנִפְסְלוּ מִן הַכֵּלִים שֶׁמְּכַבְּסִים שָׁם:


מפני הכביסה – that one can say that they (i.e., the Mikvaot/ritual baths) were lacking [in water] and were made invalid from the utensils that they would launder there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:1:10)


**וְהָרְחוֹקִים טְהוֹרִים.** שֶׁאֵין מְכַבְּסִים בָּהֶן וְלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ לְהָכִי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


הרחוקים טהורים – for they don’t launder in them but one shouldn’t be concerned for this. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:1)


**חֲלוּקִים.** שֶׁמַּטִּיף טִפִּין חֲלוּקִים כְּמִין שַׁלְשֶׁלֶת:


חלוקים – that he drips [urine] in smooth drops like a chain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:2)


**בַּתְּחִלָּה.** בִּתְחִלַּת הַטָּלַת מֵי רַגְלַיִם:


בתחילה – at the beginning of his urinating.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:3)


**טָהוֹר.** דְּלָאו מִין קֶרִי הוּא, אֶלָּא מֵעֲצִירַת הַשֶּׁתֶן הוּא בָא:


טהור – for it is not a kind of emission, but rather, from the stopping of urination it comes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:4)


**בָּאֶמְצַע וּבַסּוֹף.** שֶׁל הַטָּלַת מֵי רַגְלַיִם, טָמֵא, דְּמִין קֶרִי הוּא:


באמצע ובסוף – [in the middle or at the end] of his urination, he is impure, that he is someone with a kind of emission.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:5)


**וְנִמְשָׁכִים.** מֵי רַגְלַיִם מְדֻבָּקִים:


נמשכים (prolonged/continued or conducted in a channel) – urination that is attached.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:6)


**טָמֵא.** דְּמִין קֶרִי הוּא:


טמא – for it is a kind of emission.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:2:7)


**לְבָנִים כָּעֲכוּרִים.** וּבַתְּחִלָּה טָהוֹר, בָּאֶמְצַע וּבַסּוֹף טָמֵא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


לבנים כעכורים – and at the beginning heis pure, but in the middle and at the end he is [ritually] impure. But the Halakah is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:1)


**הַמְהַרְהֵר בַּלַּיְלָה.** וְרוֹאֶה שֶׁבָּא עַל הָאִשָּׁה, וּכְשֶׁנֵּעוֹר מִשְּׁנָתוֹ מָצָא בְשָׂרוֹ חַם וְלֹא מָצָא טִפַּת קֶרִי:


המהרהר בלילה – and he sees [in his dream] that he is coming upon (i.e., having sex with) a woman (see Talmud Niddah 43a) and when he wakes up from his sleep, he found that his flesh (i.e., his member) is hot and he didn’t find a drop of an emission.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:2)


**טָמֵא.** דְּוַדַּאי רָאָה קֶרִי אֶלָּא שֶׁנֶּאֱבַד בְּמִעוּטוֹ בַבֶּגֶד:


טמא – certainly he saw an emission but it was lost in its minuteness in the clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:3)


**הַפּוֹלֶטֶת שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי טְהוֹרָה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁשִּׁמְּשָׁה בַחֲמִישִׁי בְשַׁבָּת, וּפָלְטָה בְשַׁבָּת, לֹא שְׁנָא שִׁמְּשָׁה בִתְחִלַּת לֵיל חֲמִישִׁי דְאִשְׁתַּהֵי הַזֶּרַע בְּמֵעֶיהָ אַרְבַּע עוֹנוֹת שְׁלֵמוֹת לֵיל חֲמִישִׁי וְיוֹם חֲמִישִׁי לֵיל שִׁשִּׁי וְשִׁשִּׁי, לֹא שְׁנָא שִׁמְּשָׁה חֲמִישִׁי עִם חֲשֵׁכָה, דְּלֹא אִשְׁתַּהֵי אֶלָּא מִקְצָת חֲמִישִׁי וְלֵיל שִׁשִּׁי וְשִׁשִּׁי, דְּלֹא אִיכָּא אֶלָּא שְׁתֵּי עוֹנוֹת שְׁלֵמוֹת. שֶׁהָעוֹנָה הִיא אוֹ יוֹם אוֹ לַיְלָה:


הפולטת שכבת זרע ביום השלישי טהורה – as for example, that he had sex with her on Wednesday [night] and Friday [night] and she discharged [semen] on Shabbat, it doesn’t make a difference that he had engaged in sex with her at the beginning of Thursday night, that the semen remained in her womb four complete periods: Wednesday night, Thursday, Thursday night and Friday, nor whether he had intercourse with her on Thursday at darkness, and it did not remain in her other than part of Thursday and Thursday night and Friday, for there are only two complete periods, for the period is either the day or the night.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:4)


**רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל אוֹמֵר.** בָּא לַחֲלֹק וְלוֹמַר דְּפוֹלֶטֶת בַּשְּׁלִישִׁי לְשִׁמּוּשָׁהּ טְמֵאָה, אֲבָל פּוֹלֶטֶת בָּרְבִיעִי, טְהוֹרָה. מִיהוּ מוֹדֶה הוּא דְּמִקְצָת הַיּוֹם כְּכֻלּוֹ, דְּלֹא הִקְפִּידָה תוֹרָה אֶלָּא בְמִנְיַן הַיָּמִים וְלֹא בְמִנְיַן הָעוֹנוֹת:


ר' ישמעאל – comes to dispute and to say that she discharges [semen] on the third day [following] her intercourse, she is [ritually] impure, but if she discharges on the fourth, she is [ritually] pure. However, he admits that part of a day is considered as a complete day, for the Torah was not stringent other than in the number of days but not in the number of periods.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:5)


**וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהֵן אַרְבַּע עוֹנוֹת שְׁלֵמוֹת.** כְּגוֹן אִם שִׁמְּשָׁה בְלֵיל (רביעי) [חֲמִישִׁי] עִם חֲשֵׁכָה, פְּלִיטָתָהּ טְמֵאָה עַד תְּחִלַּת לֵיל שַׁבָּת:


ופעמים שהן ארבע עונות שלימות – as for example if he had sex with her on the night of [Thursday] at dark, her emission makes her [ritually] impure until the beginning of Friday night.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:6)


**וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהֵם חָמֵשׁ עוֹנוֹת.** כְּגוֹן אִם שִׁמְּשָׁה בִתְחִלַּת יוֹם רְבִיעִי בַּעֲלוֹת הַשַּׁחַר, וּפְלִיטָתָהּ טְמֵאָה עַד לֵיל שַׁבָּת:


ופעמים שהם חמש עונות – as for example, if he engaged in sex with her at the beginning of Wednesday at dawn, and her discharge [ of semen] is [ritually] impure until Friday night.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:7)


**פְּעָמִים שֶׁהֵן שֵׁשׁ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁשִּׁמְּשָׁה בִתְחִלַּת לֵיל רְבִיעִי וְהִיא טְמֵאָה עַד תְּחִלַּת לֵיל שַׁבָּת, נִמְצְאוּ שֵׁשׁ עוֹנוֹת שְׁלֵמוֹת:


ופעמים שהם שש – as for example, he engaged in sex with her at the beginning of Tuesday evening, and she is [ritually] impure until the beginning of Friday night, it was found to be six complete periods.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:3:8)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר לְעוֹלָם חָמֵשׁ.** עוֹנוֹת. וְאִם יָצְאָה מִקְצָת עוֹנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה, נוֹתְנִים לָהּ מִקְצָת עוֹנָה שִׁשִּׁית לְהַשְׁלִים חָמֵשׁ עוֹנוֹת, דְּכָל הַפּוֹלֶטֶת בְּתוֹךְ חָמֵשׁ עוֹנוֹת שְׁלֵמוֹת, טְמֵאָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה:


ר' עקיבא אומר לעולם חמש – [five] periods, and if it part of it came out in the first period, we give her part of the sixth period to complete the five periods, for every [woman] discharges [semen] within five complete periods is [ritually] impure. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eleazar ben Azariah.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:1)


**טְמֵאָה.** הַטִּפָּה שֶׁל שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע שֶׁפָּלְטָה:


טמאה – the drop of effusion of semen that she discharged.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:2)


**טְהוֹרָה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ טֻמְאָה דְרַבָּנָן לֵיכָּא:


טהורה – and there isn’t even the [ritual] impurity of the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:3)


**וְלֹא כִבְּדָה אֶת הַבַּיִת.** לֹא קִנְּחָה אֶת עַצְמָהּ עַד שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׁאֵר בָּהּ לַחְלוּחִית שֶׁל שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע:


ולא כבדה את הבית – did not wipe out/cleanse herself until there would not remain the moistness of the effusion of semen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:4)


**כְּאִלּוּ לֹא טָבְלָה.** דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא תִפְלֹט טִפַּת שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע שֶׁנִּשְׁאַר לָהּ בְּאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם:


כאילו לא טבלה – for we are concerned lest she emit a drop of semen that remained in her in that place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:5)


**לִכְשֶׁיָטִיל מַיִם טָמֵא.** שֶׁמָּא נִשְׁתַּיֵּר מִן הַקֶּרִי בְתוֹךְ הָאַמָּה וְיוֹצֵא עִם הַשֶּׁתֶן:


לכשיטיל מים טמא (when he does urinate he is unclean) – lest there remain from the emission within the member and he goes out with the urination.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:4:6)


**בְּיֶלֶד וּבְבָרִיא טָהוֹר.** שֶׁיּוֹצֵא בְכֹחַ וְאֵין מִשְׁתַּיֵּר. וְעַד כַּמָּה קָרוּי יֶלֶד, כָּל שֶׁעוֹמֵד עַל רַגְלוֹ אַחַת וְחוֹלֵץ מִנְעָלוֹ וְנוֹעֵל מִנְעָלוֹ, וַאֲפִלּוּ בֶן שְׁמוֹנִים שָׁנָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


בילד ובבריא טהור – that it comes out with power and does not remain. And until when is he called a child? All the while that he stands on one foot and removes his shoe and puts on his shoe, and even at age eighty. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:1)


**טְהוֹרָה מִטֻּמְאָתָהּ.** מִטֻּמְאַת נִדָּה, וּמֻתֶּרֶת לְבַעֲלָהּ:


טהורה מטומאתה – from the defilement of being a menstruant woman, and she is permitted to [have sexual relations with] her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:2)


**אֲבָל טְמֵאָה עַל גַּב רֻקָּהּ.** שֶׁנִּתְלַשׁ עַל הַמָּעוֹת קֹדֶם טְבִילָה, וּטְמֵאָה מִתּוֹרַת מַגָּע, דְּרֻקָּהּ מְטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא:


אבל טמאה על גב רוקה – as a result of the coins prior to the [ritual] immersion. And she is impure from the law of contact, for her spittle defiles through contact and carrying.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:3)


**נָתְנָה שְׂעָרָהּ בְּפִיהָ.** וְלֹא בָאוּ הַמַּיִם בִּשְׂעָרָהּ:


נתנה שערה בפיה – and the water did not come on to her hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:4)


**קָרְצָה שִׂפְתוֹתֶיהָ.** דָּחֲקָה שִׂפְתוֹתֶיהָ בְיוֹתֵר זוֹ בָזוֹ:


קרצה שפתותיה (if – in taking a ritual immersion – she pressed her lips together) – she pushed her lips greatly this one on that that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:5)


**כְּאִלּוּ לֹא טָבְלָה.** וַאֲסוּרָה לְבַעֲלָהּ כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיְתָה:


כאילו לא טבלה – and is forbidden to her husband as she was.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:6)


**הָאוֹחֵז בְּאָדָם וּבְכֵלִים וּמַטְבִּילָן טְמֵאִין.** דְּבִמְקוֹם אֲחִיזָתוֹ לֹא בָאוּ מַיִם:


האוחז באדם ובכלים ומטבילן טמאין – for in the place of his holding [them], the water did not come.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:7)


**וְאִם הֵדִיחַ יָדָיו בַּמַּיִם טְהוֹרִים.** לְפִי שֶׁהַמַּשְׁקֶה שֶׁעַל יָדָיו מִתְחַבֵּר לְמֵי הַמִּקְוֶה וְשׁוּב אֵין כָּאן חֲצִיצָה:


ואם הדיח ידיו במים טהורים – since the liquid that is on his hands combines to the waters of the Mikveh and further there is no interposition/an intervening object.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:8)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר יְרַפֶּה וְכוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר' שמעון אומר ירפה (he should loosen his hold on the objects – so that water can come in contact with them) – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:9)


**בֵּית הַסְּתָרִים.** כְּגוֹן תּוֹךְ הָאֹזֶן וְתוֹךְ הַחֹטֶם וְתוֹךְ הַפֶּה:


בית הסתרים (the covered parts of the body/posteriors) – as for example, within the ear and within the nose and within the mouth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:10)


**בֵּית הַקְּמָטִין.** שֶׁל שֶׁחִי וְשֶׁל עֶרְוָה. אִי נַמִּי, קְמָטִין שֶׁל זָקֵן וּזְקֵנָה שֶׁבְּשָׂרָם נִקְמָט:


בית הקמטין (parts of the body which have folds) – of the arm-pit and of the nakedness [of the person immersing]. Alternatively, the folds of an old man and an old woman whose flesh is folded/wrinkled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 8:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/8:5:11)


**אֵין צָרִיךְ שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ בָהֶן מָיִם.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) וְכָל אֲשֶׁר יִגַּע בּוֹ הַזָּב וְיָדָיו לֹא שָׁטַף בַּמָּיִם, וְכִי בִשְׁטִיפַת יָדַיִם נִטְהָר הַזָּב, וַהֲלֹא טְבִילַת כָּל הַגּוּף בְּמַיִם חַיִּים הוּא צָרִיךְ, אֶלָּא לוֹמַר לְךָ מַה יָּדָיו בַּנִּרְאֶה, אַף כָּל בְּשָׂרוֹ בַנִּרְאֶה, פְּרָט לְבֵית הַסְּתָרִים. וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן אָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים לְעוֹלָם יְלַמֵּד אָדָם בְּתוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ שֶׁתְּהֵא אִשָּׁה מְדִיחָה קְמָטֶיהָ בִשְׁעַת טְבִילָה, דִּנְהִי דְאֵין צְרִיכִין לָבֹא בָהֶן מַיִם, מָקוֹם הָרָאוּי לְבִיאַת מַיִם בָּעִינַן:


אין צריך שיבואו בהן מים – as it is written (Leviticus 15:11): “If one with a discharge, without having rinsed his hands in water [touches another person, that person shall wash his clothes, bathe in water, and remain impure until morning],” for with rinsing of his hands the person with gonorrhea is [ritually] purified, but isn’t immersion of all of the boy in living waters is what he needs, but rather to inform you, just as his hands are as it appears, also all of his body is as it appears, except for the covered parts of the body. But nevertheless, the Sages say that a person should always accustom in his home that woman rinses her folds hat the time of her ritual immersion, assuming that the water doesn’t have to come upon them, [but] the place that is appropriate for water to come upon it, we require it.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:1:1)


**אֵלּוּ חוֹצְצִין.** חוּטֵי צֶמֶר וְחוּטֵי פִשְׁתָּן וְהָרְצוּעוֹת. שֶׁהַבָּנוֹת מְקַלְּעוֹת בָּהֶן שֵׂעָר שֶׁבְּרֹאשָׁן, וְהֵן מְהֻדָּקוֹת בַּשֵּׂעָר שֶׁבָּרֹאשׁ וּמוֹנְעוֹת הַמַּיִם מִלִּכָּנֵס בַּשֵּׂעָר:


אלו חוצצין. חוטי צמר וחוטי פשתן והרצועות – and the daughters plait the hair that is on their head, and they are fastened in the hair that is in the head and they prevent the water from entering the hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:1:2)


**שֶׁל צֶמֶר וְשֶׁל שֵׂעָר אֵין חוֹצְצִים.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה בְּשֶׁל צֶמֶר, אֲבָל בְּחוּטִין שֶׁל שֵׂעָר מוֹדִים לוֹ חֲכָמִים, לְפִי שֶׁהַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִים בָּהֶן. וְדַוְקָא חוּטִים שֶׁבָּרֹאשׁ חוֹצְצִין. אֲבָל שֶׁל צַוָּאר, אֵין חוֹצְצִים, שֶׁאֵין אִשָּׁה חוֹנֶקֶת אֶת עַצְמָהּ:


של צמר ושל שער אין חוצצים – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda regarding wool, but threads of hair, the Sages agree with him, because the water enters in them, and especially the threads that are in the head interpose, but that of the neck do not interpose, for the woman does not chock herself.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:1)


**קִלְקֵי הַלֵּב.** שֵׂעָר שֶׁבֶּחָזֶה כְנֶגֶד הַלֵּב שֶׁנִּסְתַּבֵּךְ וְנַעֲשָׂה כְעֵין שַׁרְשְׁרוֹת, וְהֵן נִמְאָסוֹת מִן הַזֵּעָה:


קלקי הלב (matted hair on the chest) – hair that is on the chest corresponding to the heart that became entangled and made similar to chains , and they are detestable from the sweat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:2)


**וּבֵית הַסְּתָרִים בָּאִשָּׁה.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַשֵּׂעָר נֶאֱחָז וּמִסְתַּבֵּךְ בְּאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם מֵחֲמַת הַזֵּעָה וְהַטִּנּוּף וְהַלִּכְלוּךְ. וְדַוְקָא בְאִשָּׁה נְשׂוּאָה שֶׁמַּקְפֶּדֶת עַל עַצְמָהּ שֶׁלֹּא תִתְגַּנֶּה עַל בַּעֲלָהּ הוּא דְבֵית הַסְּתָרִים הָוֵי חֲצִיצָה, אֲבָל בִּפְנוּיָה דְאֵינָהּ מַקְפֶּדֶת, לֹא הָוֵי בֵית הַסְּתָרִים חֲצִיצָה:


ובית הסתרים באשה – because the hair is seized and becomes entangled at that same place on account of the sweat and the fifth and soiling . And especially with regard to a married woman who is stringent with herself so that she doesn’t become repulsive to her husband that the woman’s private parts is an interposition, but with a free woman who is not so stringent, the woman’s private parts are not an interposition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:3)


**לִפְלוּף.** צוֹאַת הָעַיִן:


לפלוף (pus sticking around the eye) – filthy matter of the eye.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:4)


**גְּלִיד.** רִיר הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַמַּכָּה שֶׁמִּתְיַבֵּשׁ וְנַעֲשָׂה גְלִיד:


גליד (scab of a wound, crust) – spittle/discharge that comes out from the wound when it dries and becomes a scab/crust.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:5)


**וּשְׂרָף הַיָּבֵשׁ.** אִם הָיָה עַל בְּשָׂרָהּ מִן הַשְּׂרָף הַנּוֹטֵף מֵהָאִילָנוֹת אוֹ מִן הַפֵּרוֹת, וְנִתְיַבֵּשׁ שָׁם:


ושרף היבש (dried sap/resin) – if it there was on her flesh from the resin that drips from the trees or from the fruit, and dried up there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:6)


**גִּלְדֵי צוֹאָה שֶׁעַל בְּשָׂרוֹ.** שֶׁנִּתְיַבְּשָׁה וְנַעֲשֵׂית גְּלִיד:


גלדי צואה שעל בשרו – that dried up and became a scab/crust.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:7)


**וְהַמִּלְמוּלִין.** כְּשֶׁיָּדָיו שֶׁל אָדָם מְלֻכְלָכוֹת בְּטִיט אוֹ בְבָצֵק אוֹ בְזֵעָה וּמוֹלֵל יָדוֹ אַחַת עַל חֲבֶרְתָּהּ נַעֲשָׂה עַל יָדוֹ כְמִין גַּרְגִּירִים שֶׁל שְׂעֹרִים:


והמלמולין (crumb-like particles of dirty or sweaty hands when rubbed against each other) – when the hands of a person are dirty with plaster or with dough or sweat and he rubs his hands one on the other, his hand becomes like grains of barley.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:8)


**בְּשֶׁל מָרֵקָה.** טִיט הַשָּׁרוּי בְּלֹבֶן בֵּיצָה, עָשׂוּי לְתַקֵּן בּוֹ הַכֵּלִים שֶׁנִּסְדְּקוּ, שֶׁאֵין הַמַּיִם מְמַחִין אוֹתוֹ. מָרֵקָה, לְשׁוֹן תַּמְרוּקֵי נָשִׁים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


בשל מריקה (a sort of earth used for polishing) – plaster/clay that is steeped in the white of an egg, if is made to repair with it a utensil that was chipped (i.e., like a putty), that the water does not polish/cleanse it. The language of מריקה is (Esther 2:12): “women’s cosmetics.” But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:9)


**יִתְדוֹת דְּרָכִים.** טִיט שֶׁנִּתְמַעֵךְ עַל יְדֵי הִלּוּךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם שֶׁמְּהַלְּכִים עָלָיו. וּכְשֶׁהוּא לַח נִקְרָא גֵץ יְוֵנִי, וּכְשֶׁהוּא יָבֵשׁ, נִקְרָא יְתַד הַדְּרָכִים, שֶׁקָּשֶׁה לְהַלֵּךְ עֲלֵיהֶן וְנִרְאֶה כִמְהַלֵּךְ עַל גַּבֵּי יְתֵדוֹת:


יתדות דרכים (way-mark of hardened clay pegs – cross-path laid out with whitened pegs of baked mud or clay) – plaster/clay that was flattened through the walking of people that walk upon it. And it is moist, it is called a white earth/chalk, but when it is dry it is called the way-mark of hardened clay pegs, that is hard to walk upon them and appears as if one is walking upon pegs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:10)


**שֶׁאֵין טוֹבְלִין בָּהֶם.** אִם הָיָה בַמִּקְוֶה טִיט כָּזֶה:


אין טובלין בהם – if plaster/clay like this was in the Mikveh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:11)


**וְלֹא מַטְבִּילִין אוֹתָן.** אִם הָיָה עַל בְּשָׂרוֹ. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים, וְלֹא מַטְבִּילִין אוֹתָם אִם נִטְמְאוּ מֵימָיו, דְּלֹא סָלְקָא הַשָּׁקָה בִמְקוֹם הַטִּיט:


ואין מטבילין אותן – if it was on his flesh. And there are those who interpret/explain that we don’t immerse them if their waters were [ritually] defiled, for contact between liquids is not effective in a place of plaster/clay.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:12)


**הַקֻּמְקְמוּס.** כְּלִי נְחֹשֶׁת שֶׁמְּחַמְּמִים בּוֹ הַמַּיִם, וּמֵחֲמַת הֶעָשָׁן וְהַשַּׁלְהֶבֶת שֶׁעוֹלָה עַל גַּבָּיו נַעֲשָׂה סְבִיבָיו פֶּחָמִין:


הקומקום (kettle) – a copper utensil in which one heats water, and on account of the smoke and the flame that goes up on its sides, it makes charcoals around it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:2:13)


**אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יְשַׁפְשֵׁף.** הַפֶּחָמִין שֶׁעַל גַּבָּיו:


אלא אם כן ישפשף (unless one scrapes) – the charcoal that is upon it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:3:1)


**וּבֵית הַסְּתָרִים בָּאִישׁ.** דְּאִישׁ אֵינוֹ מַקְפִּיד. וַאֲפִלּוּ אִשָּׁה אֵין מַקְפֶּדֶת אֶלָּא נְשׂוּאָה כְּדִפְרִישִׁית:


ובית הסתרים באיש – for a man is not stringent [concerning this part of his body]. And even a woman is not stringent unless she is married as we have explained (see Mishnah 2 above).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:3:2)


**וְשֶׁאֵינוֹ מַקְפִּיד עָלָיו אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ.** וְהוּא שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה עַל רֹב גּוּפוֹ. וּפְסַק הַהֲלָכָה לְעִנְיַן חֲצִיצָה, רֻבּוֹ וּמַקְפִּיד עָלָיו, חוֹצֵץ דְּבַר תּוֹרָה. מִעוּטוֹ, כְּלוֹמַר אִם הַדָּבָר הַחוֹצֵץ הוּא בְמִעוּט הַגּוּף, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמַּקְפִּיד עָלָיו, אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ מִן הַתּוֹרָה, אֲבָל חֲכָמִים גָּזְרוּ עַל רֻבּוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ מַקְפִּיד מִשּׁוּם רֻבּוֹ הַמַּקְפִּיד, וְעַל מִעוּטוֹ הַמַּקְפִּיד נַמִּי מִשּׁוּם רֻבּוֹ הַמַּקְפִּיד. וְלֹא גָזְרוּ עַל מִעוּטוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ מַקְפִּיד אַטּוּ מִעוּטוֹ הַמַּקְפִּיד, דְּהִיא גוּפָא גְזֵרָה, וַאֲנַן נֵיקוּם וְנִגְזֹר גְּזֵרָה לִגְזֵרָה:


ושאינו מקפיד עליו אינו חוצץ – and for him as long as it isn’t on the majority of his body. And the legal decision regarding interposition – the majority of the body and he is stringent about it, it interposes according to the words of the Torah. A minority thereof, that is to say, if the thing that interposes is in the minority of the body even though he is stringent/he is mindful of it , it does not interpose according to the words of the Torah. But the Sages decreed that on the majority [of the body] and he is not mindful about it because of the majority that he is mindful about, and on the minority that he is mindful about also because of the majority that he is mindful about. But they did not make a decree regarding the minority that he is not mindful about because of the minority that he is mindful of, for this is the body of the decree and we hold that we make a decree on a preventive measure in order to prevent the violation of another preventive measure.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:4:1)


**לִפְלוּף שֶׁבָּעַיִן.** בְּלַח אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ, בְּיָבֵשׁ, חוֹצֵץ. וְשֶׁחוּץ לָעַיִן דִּלְעֵיל, בֵּין בְּלַח בֵּין בְּיָבֵשׁ, חוֹצֵץ. וְטַעֲמָא הָוֵי, דְּחוּץ לָעַיִן אָדָם מַקְפִּיד בֵּין בְּלַח בֵּין בְּיָבֵשׁ. בְּתוֹךְ הָעַיִן, בְּיָבֵשׁ מַקְפִּיד, בְּלַח אֵינוֹ מַקְפִּיד:


לפלוף שבעין – with that which is moist that dos not interpose, [but] with dry, it interposes, for outside of the eye that is above (see Mishnah 2), whether dealing with something moist or dry, it interposes. And the reason for this is that outside of the eye, a person is mindful about it whether it is moist or whether it is dry, [whereas] within the eye, a person is mindful of something dry, but something moist, he is not mindful of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:4:2)


**כְּשׁוּת שֶׁל קָטָן.** כְּמִין שֵׂעָר נוֹלָד בִּבְשַׂר הַקְּטַנִּים, כְּעֵין שֵׂעָר הַנּוֹלָד בְּקִשּׁוּאִין שֶׁקּוֹרִין פאקו"ס בַּעֲרָבִי, וְאוֹתוֹ שֵׂעָר קָרוּי בִלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה כְּשׁוּת שֶׁל קִשּׁוּת, כָּךְ שֵׂעָר שֶׁל קָטָן הַדּוֹמֶה לוֹ קָרוּי כְשׁוּת שֶׁל קָטָן, וְהַנָּשִׁים קוֹרְאוֹת לוֹ שֵׂעָר שׁוֹטֶה:


כשות של קטן (downy hair growth of a youth before puberty) – a kind of hair that originates within the flesh of young boys , similar to the hair that originates on cucumbers that we call FAKORS in Arabic. And this hair is called the tuft/fine hairs of cucumbers in the language of the Mishnah (i.e., cucumbers or melons in an early stage when they are pubescent), such is the hair of young boys that is similar to it which is called the down hair growth of a youth before puberty, but the women call it the hair of an imbecile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:4:3)


**לֹא טָמֵא.** אִם נָגַע טֻמְאָה בְאוֹתוֹ שֵׂעָר לֹא נִטְמָא הַקָּטָן:


לא טמא – if ritual impurity did come in contact with that hair, the young child is not defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:4:4)


**וְלֹא מְטַמֵּא.** אִם הָיָה הַקָּטָן טָמֵא בְאַחַת מֵאֲבוֹת הַטֻּמְאוֹת, וְנָגַע אָדָם בְּאוֹתוֹ שֵׂעָר וְלֹא נָגַע בִּבְשָׂרוֹ, לֹא נִטְמָא הַנּוֹגֵעַ, דְּלָא חֲשִׁיב שֵׂעָר זֶה יָד לְהַכְנִיס טֻמְאָה וּלְהוֹצִיא:


ולא מטמא – if the young boy was ritually impure with one of the primary forms of ritual impurity, and a person touched that hair but did not touch his flesh, the person who touched him is not ritually impure, for this hair is not considered to be a handle to bring in ritual impurity or to remove it.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:1)


**וְהַמּוֹר.** מוסק"ו בְּלַעַ"ז. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים גּוֹרְסִים וְחֵמָר:


והמור (myrrh)– MOSKO in the foreign tongue. And there are books that have the reading "וחמר".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:2)


**דַּרְגָּשׁ.** מִטָּה קְטַנָּה. תַּרְגּוּם וְיָשַׁבְתְּ עַל מִטָּה כְבוּדָּה (יחזקאל כג), דַּרְגָּשִׁין:


דרגש (footstool in front of a high bed) – a small bed. In the Aramaic translation/Targum, (Ezekiel 23:41): “and you sat on a grand couch [with a set table in front of it -and it was My incense and My oil you laid upon it],” these are דרגשין/footstools in front of a high bed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:3)


**עַל הַנְּקִיִּים חוֹצְצִין.** מִשּׁוּם דְּקָפְדֵי עֲלֵיהֶן:


על הנקיים חוצציין – because they are mindful of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:4)


**בְּלוּסִין.** מְלֻכְלָכִים. וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ בַגְּמָרָא בְפֶרֶק כְּלָל גָּדוֹל [שַׁבָּת דַּף עו] עִסָּה בְלוּסָה:


בלוסין (utensils soaked with a mixture of colors (i.e., stains from use) – dirty. Similar to it is what is in the Gemara in the chapter, “The Great Principle”/כלל גדול [Shabbat 76b) – a dough of unsifted flour (with bran).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:5)


**אֵינָן חוֹצְצִין.** דְּלֹא קָפְדֵי עֲלֵיהּ. וְהַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא דְמִטּוֹת בַּעַל הַבַּיִת וְעָנִי:


אינן חוצצין – because they are not mindful of them. And that is the reason we are talking about the beds of an houseowner and a poor person.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:6)


**אִכּוּף.** כְּמוֹ אֻכָּף. וְהוּא שֶׁל עוֹר שֶׁמְּשִׂימִין עַל הַחֲמוֹר כְּשֶׁנּוֹשֵׂא הַמַּשָּׂא:


איכוף – like an אוכף/saddle. And it is [made] of leather and we place it on the donkey when it carries a burden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:7)


**זַקָּקִין.** נוֹשְׂאֵי הַנּוֹדוֹת שֶׁל עוֹר. לְשׁוֹן זִיקָא בֵּין מַלְיָא בֵין חֲסֵרָה שָׁרְיָא, בְּמַסֶּכֶת עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה (דף ס):


זקקין (saddles used by dealers in hose) – those who carry the leather bottles/skins. The language of hose/זיקא whether the tied-up wine skin was filled entirely or whether it was lacking, is permitted in Tractate Avodah Zarah 60a.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:8)


**אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ.** שֶׁאֵין מַקְפִּידִין עַל הַלִּכְלוּךְ:


אינו חוצץ – for they are not mindful about the dirtiness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:5:9)


**עַד כְּאִסָּר הָאִיטַלְקִי.** שֶׁהוּא רֹחַב כְּאַרְבָּעָה גַרְגְּרֵי שְׂעֹרָה, חוֹצֵץ. פָּחוֹת מִכָּאן, אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא לֹא יָהֵב בֵּיהּ שִׁעוּרָא, אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ פָחוֹת מִכְּאִסָּר חוֹצֵץ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


עד כאיסר האיטלקי – which is wide like four grains of barley, it interposes; less than this it does not interpose. But the first Tanna/teacher does not give a measurement, but even less than an Issar, it interposes. And the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:6:1)


**מִשְּׁנֵי צְדָדִים.** שֶׁעוֹבֵר מִשְּׁנֵי עֶבְרֵי הַבֶּגֶד:


משני צדדין – that it passes over from two sides of the clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:6:2)


**שֶׁל בַּנָּאִים.** תַּלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים שֶׁעוֹסְקִים בְּבִנְיָנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם. וְקָפְדֵי עַל רְבָב שֶׁבְּבִגְדֵיהֶם, דְּאָמַר רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן כָּל תַּלְמִיד חָכָם שֶׁנִּמְצָא רְבָב עַל בְּגָדָיו חַיָּב מִיתָה:


של בנאים – Sages who engage in the building of the world and are mindful of the greasy stains that are on their clothing, for Rabbi Yohanan stated (Talmud Shabbat 114a): Any Sage upon whom grease is found on his clothing is liable for death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:6:3)


**וְשֶׁל בּוּר.** גָּרוּעַ מֵעַם הָאָרֶץ. וְלֹא קָפֵיד עַל לִכְלוּךְ שֶׁבִּבְגָדָיו אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יוֹצֵא מֵעֵבֶר לְעֵבֶר. וּמַרְדַּעַת דַּאֲמַרַן לְעֵיל דְּחוֹצֵץ, הַיְנוּ שֶׁעוֹבֵר מִשְּׁנֵי צְדָדִין. דְּלֹא תְהֵא מַרְדַּעַת חֲשׁוּבָה מִבִּגְדוֹ שֶׁל עַם הָאָרֶץ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ושל בור – worse than a common, uneducated person (i.e. more correctly, someone who is not meticulous in his observance of the commandments), for he is not mindful of the dirty that is in his clothes unless it comes out from side to side, and the pack-saddle or cushion that we stated above (i.e., see the previous Mishnah) that it interposes, that is when it passes from both sides. So that there should not be a pack-saddle that is more important than the clothing of a common, uneducated person. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:7:1)


**זַפָּתִין.** זוֹפְתֵי חָבִיּוֹת. אִי נַמִּי, עוֹשֵׂי הַזֶּפֶת:


זפתין (pitch/asphalt workers) – those who apply pitch to barrels. Alternatively, those who make the pitch/asphalt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:7:2)


**מְפַסְּלֵי אִילָנוֹת.** שֶׁנּוֹטְלִים הַפְּסֹלֶת מִן הָאִילָנוֹת. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, כּוֹרְתֵי הָאִילָנוֹת. לְשׁוֹן פְּסָל לְךָ, שֶׁכּוֹרֵת הָעֲנָפִים מִן הָאִילָן כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְעַבֶּה וְיַגְדִּיל גּוּפוֹ שֶׁל אִילָן:


מפסלי אילנות (trimmers of trees) – that take the refuse from the trees. Another explanation: Those who cut down the trees. The language of (Exodus 34:1): “Carve [for yourself] (two tablets of stone like the first),” that he cuts the branches from the tree in order that it should become thick and that the body of the tree will grow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/9:7:3)


**שֶׁל קַיָּצִין.** הַשּׁוֹטְחִים לַחַמָּה לְיַבֵּשׁ פֵּרוֹת שֶׁל קַיִץ. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, מְלַקְּטֵי קוֹצִין. וְכָל הָנֵי לֹא קָפְדִי אַלִּכְלוּךְ הַמִּטְפָּחוֹת שֶׁלָּהֶם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


של קייצין – who spread them out in the sun to dry out the fruit of the summer. Another explanation: those who collect the summer fruit. But all of these are not mindful regarding the dirtiness of their aprons. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:1)


**כָּל יְדוֹת הַכֵּלִים.** כְּגוֹן יַד הַקַּרְדֹּם וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָהּ שֶׁהִכְנִיסָהּ בַּבַּרְזֶל:


כל ידות הכלים – as for example, the handle of hatchet/mattock and those of a similar manner he inserted it with an iron tool.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא כְדַרְכָּהּ.** בְּעִקּוּם:


שלא כדרכה – in a curve.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:3)


**וְלֹא מֵרְקָן.** לֹא גָמַר הַכְנָסָתָן. לְשׁוֹן וּמֵרַק אַחַר שְׁחִיטָה עַל יָדוֹ, בְּמַסֶּכֶת יוֹמָא [דַּף לב]:


ולא מירקן (but did not finish them off – so that it fitted accurately) – but he did not complete their insertion. It is the language of "ומירק אחר שחיטה על ידו" /and another completed the ritual slaughter on his behalf, in Tractate Yoma 32b (but also in the Mishnah found in Tractate Yoma 31b or Chapter 3, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:4)


**אוֹ שֶׁמֵּרְקָן.** שֶׁגָּמַר הַכְנָסָתָן וְנִשְׁבְּרוּ:


או שמירקן – that he completed their insertion but they broke.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:5)


**כְּלִי שֶׁהִטְבִּילוֹ דֶרֶךְ פִּיו כְּאִלּוּ לֹא טָבָל.** דְּכָל כְּלִי שֶׁבִּתְחִלַּת הַכְנָסָתוֹ בַמַּיִם כּוֹפֵהוּ עַל פִּיו, אֵין הַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִין לְתוֹכוֹ לְעוֹלָם אֲפִלּוּ מַכְנִיסוֹ כֻלּוֹ, עַד שֶׁיַּטֶּנּוּ:


כלי שהטבילו דרך פיו כאילו לא טבל – for every utensil that at the beginning of its insertion in water, he turns it upside down/inverts it on its mouth, the water does not enter in it ever even if he inserts all of it until he turns it [on its side].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:6)


**בְּלֹא הַזִּבּוֹרִית.** שֶׁלֹּא הִטְבִּיל בֵּית יָדוֹ, אוֹ דָבָר נוֹסָף שֶׁיֵּשׁ בַּכְּלִי בְאַחַת מִקְּצוֹתָיו. וּלְהָכִי קָרוּ לֵיהּ זִבּוֹרִית, שֶׁאֵינוֹ עִקָּרוֹ שֶׁל כְּלִי. וְאֵין הַמַּיִם בָּאִין בָּהֶן עַד שֶׁיַּטֶּנּוּ עַל צִדּוֹ. וְכָל הָנֵי דְמַתְנִיתִין כֻּלְּהוּ טַעֲמָא [כְּדֵי] שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ הַמַּיִם בְּכֻלָּן:


בלא הזיבורית (if the vessel to be immersed has no saucer – a rim at the bottom, wider than the belly of the vessel, and which forces the water into the belly) – that he did not immerse the handle or something additional that is the utensil has on one of its ends, and for this they call it זיבורית that it is not of the essence of the utensil, and the water does not come on them until he turns it on its side. And all of these of our Mishnah the reason for all of them is that water will come on all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:1:7)


**קַלְמָרִין.** כְּלִי שֶׁמַּנִּיחִין בּוֹ אֶת הַדְּיוֹ. וְכֵן קוֹרִים אוֹתוֹ בִלְשׁוֹן רוֹמִי קלמר"ו. וְיֵשׁ מֵהֶן שֶׁפִּיהֶן שׁוֹקֵעַ לְתוֹכָן כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׁפֵךְ הַדְּיוֹ אֲפִלּוּ אִם יֵהָפֵךְ עַל פִּיו. וְאִם הִטְבִּילוֹ כְדַרְכּוֹ, אֵין הַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִים בֶּחָלָל הַשָּׁקוּעַ שֶׁסְּבִיב פִּיו מִבִּפְנִים, לְפִיכָךְ צָרִיךְ לְנַקֵּב מִן הַצַּד:


קלמרין (inkstand – of ordinary men with a rim bent inside to prevent spilling) – a utensil that one places in it the ink. And similarly, we call it in the Roman language KALMARO. And there are of them where their mouths are sunk within them in order that the ink does not spill, even if it will be turned upside down on its mouth. But if he immersed it in its appropriate manner, the water does not enter into the sunken space that surrounds the mouth from inside, therefore, one needs to make an incision from the side.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:1)


**הַכַּר.** הוּא שֶׁשּׁוֹכֵב עָלָיו:


הכר (mattress)– that he lies down upon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:2)


**הַכֶּסֶת.** הוּא שֶׁמֵּשִׂים תַּחַת מְרַאֲשׁוֹתָיו:


הכסת (pillow [cases] of leather) – that he places underneath his head.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:3)


**שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ בָהֶן הַמָּיִם.** לְתוֹכָן. שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ כַּר וְכֶסֶת שֶׁל עוֹר לְהַכְנִיס וּלְהוֹצִיא מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָן:


שיבואו בהן המים – inside them. For it is the manner of the mattress and pillow cases of leather to insert and to remove what is within them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:4)


**כֶּסֶת עֲגֻלָּה.** וּקְטַנָּה שֶׁעוֹשִׂים הַשָּׂרִים תַּחַת מְרַאֲשׁוֹתֵיהֶן:


כסת עגולה – [round] and small [pillows] that the princes make under their heds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:5)


**הַכַּדּוּר.** פלוט"א:


הכדור – PALUTA


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:6)


**וְהָאֵמוּם.** דְּפוּס שֶׁעוֹשִׂים עָלָיו אֶת הַמִּנְעָל. וְהוּא עָשׂוּי מֵעוֹר וּמְמַלְּאִים אוֹתוֹ בְשֵׂעָר אוֹ בְמוֹכִין:


והאמום (shoe-maker’s last) – the frame/mold that they make upon it the shoe. And it is made of leather and they fill it with hair or hackled wool (i.e., to soften them).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:7)


**וְהַקָּמֵעַ.** שֶׁל כְּתָב אוֹ שֶׁל עִקָּרִין מְחֻפֶּה עוֹר:


והקמיע (and an amulet) – of writing [of names/verses] or sterile covered with leather.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:8)


**וּתְפִלָּה.** שֶׁל יָד אוֹ שֶׁל רֹאשׁ:


ותפלה – either of the hand or of the head.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:2:9)


**אֵינָן צְרִיכִים שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ בָהֶן הַמָּיִם.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לְהַכְנִיס וּלְהוֹצִיא מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָן:


אינו צריך שיבואו בהן מים – for it is not the practice to insert or to remove what is within them (i.e., parchments).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:1)


**קִשְׁרֵי הֶעָנִי.** בְּגָדָיו שֶׁל עָנִי שֶׁנִּקְרְעוּ וּקְשָׁרָן, הַקֶּשֶׁר הַהוּא אֵינוֹ עָשׂוּי לְהַתִּיר לְעוֹלָם, הִלְכָּךְ אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהַתִּירוֹ בִשְׁעַת טְבִילָה כְּדֵי שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ עָלָיו מַיִם:


קשרי העני – the clothing of a poor man that were torn and knotted, this knot is not made to be release/undone ever, therefore one does not need to loosened it at the time of ritual immersion in order that the water would come upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:2)


**וְהַנִּימִין.** נִימִין וּפְתִילִין שֶׁתּוֹלִין עַל שְׂפַת הַסָּדִין לְנוֹי וְעוֹשִׂין בָּהֶן קְשָׁרִים, וְאֵינָן עֲשׂוּיִין לְהַתִּיר לְעוֹלָם:


הנימין (cord,string) – the cords/string and the twisted threads on the rim of the sheet for beauty and they make knots with them, and they are not made to be loosened ever.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:3)


**וַחֲבַט שֶׁל סַנְדָּל.** אָזְנַיִם שֶׁיֵּשׁ לִרְצוּעוֹת שֶׁל סַנְדָּל, אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהַתִּיר הַקֶּשֶׁר שֶׁלָּהֶן בִּשְׁעַת טְבִילָה, שֶׁאֵין אוֹתוֹ קֶשֶׁר עָשׂוּי לְהַתִּיר:


חבט של סנדל (thongs of a sandal joined in a knot) – the handles that the straps of a sandal have, one does not have to loosen their knot at the time of ritual immersion, for this knot is not made to be loosened.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:4)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא חוֹצָה.** שֶׁהַקֶּשֶׁר חוֹצֵץ, שֶׁאֵין הַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִים לְתוֹכָהּ. וְאֵימָתַי יִהְיֶה זֶה, כְּשֶׁהַקֶּשֶׁר מְהֻדָּק יָפֶה וְאָז אֵינוֹ עָשׂוּי לְהַתִּיר. וּלְפִי שֶׁהוּא עָשׂוּי לְהִתְקַיֵּם, אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהַתִּירוֹ בִשְׁעַת טְבִילָה:


בזמן שהיא חוצה – that the knot interposes, that the water does not enter into it. And when will this be? At the time when the knot is fastened well, and then it is not made to be loosened. But since it is made to last, one does not need to release/loosen it at the time of ritual immersion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:5)


**וְשֶׁל זְרוֹעַ.** קֶשֶׁר שֶׁל תְּפִלָּה שֶׁל יָד:


ושל זרוע – the knot of the phylactery/tefillin of the hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:6)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵינָהּ עוֹלָה וְיוֹרֶדֶת.** דְּמִהַדַּק הַקֶּשֶׁר הַרְבֵּה, וְאֵין הָרְצוּעָה עוֹלָה וְיוֹרֶדֶת בּוֹ:


בשמן שאינה עולה ויורדת – and the knot is fasted a great deal and the strap does not ascend or descend upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:7)


**וְאָזְנֵי הַחֵמֶת.** אָזְנֵי הַנּוֹד יֵשׁ בָּהֶם קְשָׁרִים מְהֻדָּקִין:


ואזני החמת (handgrip of a waterskin) – the handgrip of the leather bottle that in them tight connections.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:3:8)


**תַּרְמוּל.** כִּיס גָּדוֹל שֶׁל עוֹר שֶׁנּוֹשְׂאִין הָרוֹעִים. וּבִלְשׁוֹן מִקְרָא קָרוּי יַלְקוּט:


תורמל – a large leather pouch that the shepherds carry. And in the language of Scripture it is called a ילקוט/receptacle, wallet (see First Samuel, Chapter 17, Verse 40: “[He took his stick, picked a few smooth stones from the wadi,] put them in the pocket of his shepherd’s bag [and sling in hand, he went toward the Philistine].”



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:1)


**פְּרַקְסִין.** לְבוּשׁ הַתַּחְתּוֹן שֶׁעַל הַבָּשָׂר, וְהוּא פָתוּחַ בַּכְּתֵפַיִם, וּכְשֶׁלּוֹבְשׁוֹ קוֹשְׁרוֹ עַל הַכָּתֵף, וּבִשְׁעַת הֶפְשֵׁט מַתִּירוֹ:


פרקסין (underwear/shirt) – the underclothes that are upon the skin, and this is open at the shoulders, and when he wears it, he ties it on the shoulder, and at the time of removal/stripping, he loosens it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:2)


**וְשָׂפָה שֶׁל סָדִין צָרִיךְ לְמַתֵּחַ.** הַקְּמָטִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ בִּשְׂפָתוֹ הָעֶלְיוֹנָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ הַמַּיִם בֵּין אוֹתָם הַקְּמָטִים:


ושפה של סדין צריך למתה – the folds/creases that are in the upper rim/hem in order that the water can come between those folds/creases.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:3)


**לְמַתֵּחַ.** לְפָשְׁטָן וּלְמָשְׁכָן. כְּמוֹ וַיִּמְתָּחֵם כָּאֹהֶל לָשָׁבֶת (ישעיה מ):


למתח (to stretch) – to stretch them and to draw them out/extend them, like (Isaiah 40:22): “Stretched them out like a tent to dwell in.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:4)


**וּשְׁנָצִין שֶׁל סַנְדָּל.** רְצוּעוֹת שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרוֹ בָהֶן:


ושנץ של סנדל (laces of a sandal) – straps that one ties/knots them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:4:5)


**שֶׁיְּבַעְבֵּעוּ.** כְּשֶׁמַּטְבִּיל אָדָם בֶּגֶד בְּמַיִם, עוֹלִין כְּמִין אֲבַעְבּוּעוֹת בַּמַּיִם. וּכְשֶׁהֵן מְכֻבָּסִים וּמַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ עֲלֵיהֶן, מִשֶּׁהִתְחִילוּ הַמַּיִם לְבַעְבֵּעַ, טָהֲרוּ, לְפִי שֶׁהֻשְּׁקוּ הַמַּיִם הַבְּלוּעִים בּוֹ לְמֵי הַמִּקְוֶה. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהֵן נְגוּבִים אֵין הַמַּיִם בָּאִין בְּכֻלּוֹ עַד שֶׁיָּנוּחוּ מִבִּעְבּוּעָן. שֶׁכְּשֶׁאָדָם מַטְבִּיל סָדִין וְאֵין יָכוֹל לְשָׁטְחוֹ שֶׁאֵין הַמִּקְוֶה רְחָבָה כָל כָּךְ, תּוֹחֲבוֹ בַמַּיִם כְּשֶׁהוּא מְקֻפָּל וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ מְבַעְבְּעִים הַמַּיִם, וּכְשֶׁנָּחִין מִבִּעְבּוּעָן בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁבָּאוּ הַמַּיִם בְּכֻלּוֹ:


עד שיבעבעו (of garments dipped in water until they are soaked thru and cease from bulging) – when a person immerses clothing in the water, they emerge like kinds of bubbles/bulges and when they are launder and liquid drips from them, when the water begins to bulge, they have been purified, because they have been in contact with the water that has been absorbed by them to the waters of the Mikveh, but when they are dried, the water does not come into all of it until they have ceased from bulging. That when a person immerses a sheet and is not able to stretch it out, for the Mikveh is not that wide, he inserts it in the water when it is folded up, and through it the water bulges/bubbles, but when they rest from bulging it is known that the waters came through all of it.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:5:1)


**מַטְבִּילָן עַד מְקוֹם הַמִּדָּה.** וְהַשְּׁאָר אֵין צָרִיךְ טְבִילָה, דְּכָל הָעוֹמֵד לֵחָתֵךְ כְּחָתוּךְ דָּמֵי. וְלֹא הָוֵי חֲצִיצָה לִמְקוֹם הַחֲתָךְ עַצְמוֹ, דְּבֵית הַסְּתָרִים דְּכֵלִים אֵין טָעוּן בִּיאַת מַיִם כְּמוֹ בְאָדָם. וִיתֵרִים בֵּית הַסְּתָרִים דְּכֵלִים עַל בֵּית הַסְּתָרִים דְּאָדָם, דְּאִלּוּ בֵית הַסְּתָרִים דְּאָדָם נְהִי דְאֵין טְעוּנִים בִּיאַת מַיִם בָּעִינַן רָאוּי לְבִיאַת מַיִם, וּבֵית הַסְּתָרִים דְּכֵלִים אֲפִלּוּ רָאוּי לְבִיאַת מַיִם לֹא צְרִיכֵי, דְּאֵין אָדָם מַקְפִּיד שָׁם:


מטבילן עד מקום המדה (one immerses them up to the point of their proper measure) – and the rest does not require ritual immersion, for all that are stand to be cut off is considered as something cut off. And there is no interposition to the place of the cutting itself, for the covered parts of utensils do not require being in water like that of a human being, and they permit the covered parts of utensils on the covered parts of humans, for whereas the covered parts of a person, assuming that they don’t require water coming on them, we do need that they are worthy for water to come on them, but the covered pars of utensils, even if they are worthy for water to come upon them, it is not necessary, for a person is not particular/mindful there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:5:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה אומר כו' – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:5:3)


**מַטְבִּילָן עַד מְקוֹם הַמִּדָּה.** עַד אַרְבָּעָה לְגָדוֹל וְעַד עֲשָׂרָה לְקָטָן, וְהַשְּׁאָר טָהוֹר וְאֵין צָרִיךְ טְבִילָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ כָלְתָה מִדָּה לַחֲצִי טַבַּעַת שֶׁבַּשַּׁלְשֶׁלֶת אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהַטְבִּיל אֶלָּא עַד חֲצִי טַבַּעַת:


מטבילין עד מקום המדה – until [a length] of four [handbreadths] for a large [bucket] and up to ten [handbreadths] for a small [bucket], and the remainder is ritually pure and does not require ritual immersion, and even if the measure ended at half of ring in the chain, there is no need to immerse other than up until one-half of the ring.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:5:4)


**רַבִּי טַרְפוֹן אוֹמֵר עַד שֶׁיַּטְבִּיל אֶת כָּל הַטַּבָּעַת.** שֶׁכָּלְתָה הַמִּדָּה לְחֶצְיוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי טַרְפוֹן:


ר' טרפון אומר עד שיטבול את כל הטבעת – for the measure had ended at its half. And the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Tarfon.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:1)


**אֵין מַטְבִּילִין חַמִּין בְּצוֹנֵן.** דָּאֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִין אֵין לָהֶם טָהֳרָה בַמִּקְוֶה, אֶלָּא הַמַּיִם שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ בִלְבַד, מִטַּעַם הַשָּׁקָה וְחִבּוּר, וְאֵין הַשָּׁקָה מוֹעֶלֶת אֶלָּא בְמִין עִם מִינוֹ, חַמִּין בְּחַמִּין וְיָפִים בְּיָפִים וּמָרִים בְּמָרִים. אֲבָל מִין בְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ מִינוֹ אֵין הַשָּׁקָה מוֹעֶלֶת בָּהֶן וְלֹא נִטְהֲרוּ הַמַּיִם:


אין מטבילין חמין בצונן – that food and liquid do not have ritual purity in a Mikveh, but rather, only the water that had been defiled only, because of contact and connection between liquids, but contact between liquids is only effective in cases of one [kind of liquid] mixed with something similar, warm with warm, fresh [water] with fresh [water] and bitter [water] with bitter [water], but one type mixed with another type, the contact between liquids is not effective and the waters were not purified.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:2)


**מָלֵא מַשְׁקִין.** כְּגוֹן יַיִן וְשֶׁמֶן וּמֵי פֵרוֹת וְכָל מַשְׁקִין חוּץ מִמַּיִם:


מלא משקין – as for example, wine and oil and fruit juice and all liquids other than water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:3)


**כְּאִלּוּ לֹא טָבָל.** מִשּׁוּם דְּחָיְצֵי. וְדַוְקָא שֶׁלֹּא נִשְׁתַּנּוּ מַרְאֵיהֶן, שֶׁלֹּא נִתְבַּטְּלוּ בַמַּיִם:


כאילו לא טבל – because they interpose. And especially, that their appearance didn’t change, that they were not abolished in the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:4)


**מָלֵא מֵי רַגְלַיִם רוֹאִין אוֹתָן כְּאִלּוּ הֵן מָיִם.** דְּמֵי רַגְלַיִם מִין מַיִם הֵן, וּכְשֶׁהִשִּׁיקוּ עֲלֵיהֶן מֵי מִקְוֶה נַעֲשׂוּ חִבּוּר לָהֶן וְלֹא חָיְצֵי וְנִטְהַר הַכְּלִי. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים שֶׁגּוֹרְסִין, רוֹאִין אוֹתָן כְּאִלּוּ הֵן יַיִן, וְאִם הָיוּ מִשְׁתַּנִּים מַרְאֵיהֶן וְחוֹזְרִים לְמַרְאֵה מַיִם, מִתְבַּטְּלִים בַּמַּיִם וְנִטְהָר הַכְּלִי. וּכְגִרְסָא זוֹ מוּכָח בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא:


מלא מי רגלין רואין אותן כאילו הן מים – for urine is a kind of water, and when the waters of the Mikveh come in contact with them, a connection is made to them, and they do not interpose and the utensil is purified. But there are books that have the reading: "רואין אותן כאילו הם יין"/we see them as if they are wine, but if their appearance would change and they return to the appearance of water, they are nullified in the water and the utensil is purified. And like this reading is found in the Tosefta [Mikvaot, Chapter 7, Halakha 4].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:5)


**מָלֵא מֵי חַטָּאת.** מַיִם שֶׁנִּתְקַדְּשׁוּ בְאֵפֶר פָּרָה, וּמֵהֶם מַזִּים עַל טְמֵאֵי מֵתִים:


מלא מי חטאת – water that had been sanctified in the ashes of the [Red] Heifer. And from them we sprinkle on those who are [ritually] impure through contact with the dead.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:6)


**עַד שֶׁיִּרְבּוּ הַמַּיִם עַל מֵי חַטָּאת.** שֶׁיִּהְיֶה רֹב הַכְּלִי פָנוּי כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּרְבּוּ מֵי מִקְוֶה הַנִּכְנָסִים בּוֹ עַל מֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁהָיוּ בְתוֹכוֹ. וְאִם לָאו, עֲדַיִן הוּא בְטֻמְאָתוֹ וַהֲרֵי הֵן כִּשְׁאָר מַשְׁקִין שֶׁחוֹצְצִין בֵּין הַכְּלִי וּבֵין מֵי הַמִּקְוֶה:


עד ירבו המים על מי חטאת – that most of the utensil will be empty in order that the waters of the Mikveh that enter the utensil will be greater than the waters of purification [from the Red Heifer] that would be within it. For if not, he still remains in his [ritual] defilement and they (i.e., the waters) are like the rest of the liquids that interpose between the utensil and the waters of the Mikveh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:7)


**כּוֹר.** שְׁלֹשִׁים סְאִין:


כור – thirty Seah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:8)


**וְאֵין בּוֹ אֶלָּא רְבִיעִית.** מֵי חַטָּאת. אוֹ שְׁאָר מַשְׁקִין:


ואין בו אלא רביעית – waters of purification [from the Red Heifer], or the rest of the liquids.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:6:9)


**כְּאִלּוּ לֹא טָבָל.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי, רְבִיעִית מַשְׁקִין אוֹ רְבִיעִית מֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַכְּלִי, פּוֹסְלִין בִּטְבִילָה, וְחוֹצְצִין אֲפִלּוּ בְתוֹךְ כְּלִי גָדוֹל, עַד שֶׁיָּסִיר הָרְבִיעִית שֶׁל מַשְׁקִין אוֹ שֶׁל מֵי חַטָּאת וְיַחֲזֹר וְיַטְבִּילוֹ רֵיקָן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


כאילו לא טבל – for Rabbi Yossi holds that a quarter [LOG] of liquids or a quarter [LOG] of waters of purification [from the Red Heifer], invalidate ritual immersion, and interpose even within a large utensil, until he removes the quarter [LOG] of liquids or of the waters of purification [from the Red Heifer] and he returns and immerses them empty. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:7:1)


**כָּל הָאֳכָלִים מִצְטָרְפִין.** אָכַל אֳכָלִים טְמֵאִים מִכַּמָּה מִינִים וְיֵשׁ בְּכֻלָּן כַּחֲצִי פְרָס שֶׁהֵן שְׁתֵּי בֵיצִים לְרַשִׁ"י וּבֵיצָה וּמֶחֱצָה לְדִבְרֵי רַמְבַּ"ם, מִצְטָרְפִין לִפְסֹל גְּוִיָּתוֹ מִלֶּאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה. וְכֵן שָׁתָה מִכַּמָּה מִינִים שֶׁל שִׁבְעָה מַשְׁקִין שֶׁהֵם מַיִם יַיִן שֶׁמֶן דְּבַשׁ חָלָב דָּם טַל, שֶׁאֵלּוּ בִלְבַד נִקְרָאִין מַשְׁקִין, כֻּלָּן מִצְטָרְפִין זֶה עִם זֶה לִפְסֹל אֶת הַגְּוִיָּה בִּרְבִיעִית הַלֹּג, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) מִכָּל הָאֹכֶל אֲשֶׁר יֵאָכֵל וְכָל מַשְׁקֶה אֲשֶׁר יִשָּׁתֶה, לִמֵּד עַל הָאֳכָלִין שֶׁכֻּלָּן מִצְטָרְפִין וְעַל מַשְׁקִין שֶׁכֻּלָּן מִצְטָרְפִין:


כל האוכלים מצטרפין – [if] one consume impure foods from several kinds and there is in all of them like one-half of a furrow (i.e., a field of a square P’ras, declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over it from a ploughed grave) which is two eggs [bulk] according to Rashi and an egg-and-a-half according to the words of Maimonides (see also Tractate Meilah, Chapter 4, Mishnah 5), they combine to invalidate his body from consuming heave-offering [if he is a Kohen]. And similarly, if he drank from several kinds of the seven liquids which are water, wine, olive oil, honey, milk, blood and dew, for these alone are called liquids. All of these combine one with the other to invalidate the body with one-quarter of a LOG, as it is written (Leviticus 11:34): “As to any food that may be eaten, it shall become unclean if it came in contact with water, or to any liquid that may be drunk, [it shall become unclean if it was inside any vessel],” it teaches on the food-stuffs that they all combine and on all the liquids that all of them combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:7:2)


**שֶׁעָשׂוּ בוֹ שְׁאָר מַשְׁקִין.** לִפְסֹל אֶת הַגְּוִיָּה:


שעשו בו שאר משקין – to invalidate the body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:7:3)


**כַּמָּיִם.** וְאֵין שְׁאָר מַשְׁקִין פּוֹסְלִין אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין כַּמַּיִם. אִי נַמִּי, אֵין שְׁאָר מַשְׁקִין מַשְׁלִימִין שִׁעוּר מִקְוֶה לְאַרְבָּעִים סְאָה כַּמַּיִם, דִּכְתִיב מִקְוֵה מַיִם, וְלֹא מִקְוֶה שֶׁל שְׁאָר מַשְׁקִין:


כמים – but the rest of the liquids invalidate the Mikveh with three LOG like water. Alternatively, the rest of the liquids complete the measurement of the Mikveh for forty Seah like water, as it is written (Leviticus 11:36): “[However, a spring or cistern] in which water is collected [shall be clean],” but not a Mikveh of the rest of the liquids.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:1)


**שָׁתָה מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין.** שְׁאָר מַשְׁקִין חוּץ מִמַּיִם:


שתה משקין טמאין – other liquids other than water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:2)


**שֶׁאֵינָן טְהוֹרִים בַּגּוּף.** לֹא עָלְתָה לָהֶן טְבִילָה בִּטְבִילַת הַגּוּף, דָּאֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין חוּץ מִמַּיִם אֵין לָהֶם טָהֳרָה בַמִּקְוֶה.


שאינן טהורים בגוף – the [ritual] immersion did not count for them through the immersion of the body, for food and liquid outside of water do not have ritual purity in the Mikveh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:3)


**אֲבָל שָׁתָה מַיִם טְמֵאִין וְטָבַל.** טְהוֹרִים בַּגּוּף. שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶן טָהֳרָה בַמִּקְוֶה עַל יְדֵי הַשָּׁקָה:


אבל שתה מים טמאין וטבל – they are [ritually] pure in the body, and they have purity in the Mikveh through contact between liquids [placing iit in a container and submerging the container in a ritual bath so that the water of the ritual bath comes in contact with the impure water].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:4)


**הֲרֵי הִיא כְמוֹת שֶׁהָיְתָה.** טְהוֹרָה כְמוֹת שֶׁהָיְתָה, שֶׁהוּא מַצִּיל עָלֶיהָ בְעוֹדָהּ בְּלוּעָה שֶׁלֹּא תִטַּמָּא:


הרי הוא כמות שהיתה – pure like it was, that saves it while it is still absorbed so that it would not become defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:5)


**טוֹבֵל וְאוֹכֵל בַּתְּרוּמָה.** דְּטֻמְאָה בְלוּעָה אֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה אֲחֵרִים:


טובל ואוכל בתרומה – for impurity that is absorbed doe not defile others.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:6)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהוּא נִרְאֶה.** עַל גּוּפוֹ מִבַּחוּץ:


בזמן שהוא נראה – on his body from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Mikvaot 10:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Mikvaot/r/10:8:7)


**טוֹבֵל וְאוֹכֵל בַּתְּרוּמָה.** לֹא שְׁנָא חֵץ טָמֵא וְלֹא שְׁנָא חֵץ טָהוֹר, אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ בַּטְּבִילָה כָּל זְמַן שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה:


טובל ואוכל בתרומה – it doesn’t make a difference whether it is an impure arrow or whether it is a pure arrow, it does not interpose through [ritual] immersion all the while that it is not seen.
